7a1(1): @#//lus kyis phyag byas de nas ni//gtso bo rdo rje sems dpa' la//lags so zhes bya byin nas ni//tshangs dbyangs zab mo snyan pa yin//'gro ba'i mgon 
7a2(1): pos gsungs pa yis//rnal 'byor chen po'i rgyud dpal rdo rje 'phreng ba mngon par rtogs las gsol ba btab pa dang dri dang//slob ma'i la sogs brgyud kyi gleng gzhi'i le'u bya ba ste dang pa'o////
7a3(1): de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rjes gsungs//de nyid gsungs pa'i 'gro mgon gyis//lag na rdo rje la bos te//yang dag dris ba'i nges pa bshad//snying po yi ni don bsdus pa//dbye ba dka' 
7a4(1): ba'i dngos grub po kun//khyod kyi dri ba byas pa rnams//rdo rje'i bdag phyug 'chang chen bdag/bya ba spyod pa'i rgyud rnams dang//rnal 'byor rgyud rnams gang du'ang//mchog tu gsal po ma 
7a5(1): gsungs 'di//rdo rje dbang phyug de nyid mchog/dris pa'i lan rnams gsal por ni//nges par nga yin rab bshad bya//skad cig gis ni sbyin phye la//mchog tu smad 'byung ston mdzad pa//
7a6(1): thugs rjes khyab pas de skad ces gsungs//de phyir khyod ni lags par nyon//de nas bcom ldan thugs rjes thugs//brlan zhing the tshom gcod pa la//phyag na rdo rjes gsol btab ste//
7a7(1): mchog tu ba'i tshig gis bshad pa gsungs pa ni//dbang bskur ba ni gtso bo ste//dngos grub thams cad rtag tu gnas//nga yis ji bzhin don bshad kyis//de phyir thog mar yang 
7b1(1): dag nyin//thog mar slob ma gang gi tshe//blo ldan yang dag dbang bskur na//rdzogs pa'i rim pa'i rnal 'byor la//de'i tshe na snon du 'gyur//yang dag dbang bskur gyis dben na//
7b2(1): bsgrub pos rgyud kyi don shes kyang //slob dpon slob mar mtshungs par ni//mi zad dmyal ba chen por 'gro//de phyir yang dag bla ma ni//rab tu gus pas bsnyen bkur bya//dbang bskur thob cing 
7b3(1): de nyid shes//blo ldan snying rje'i bdag nyid can//zab cing rgya che dri ma med//gnod pa'i cing 'tshe ba rnam par spangs//lus kyis yan lag rdzogs pa dang//mtshan dang dpe byad rnams dang ldan//
7b4(1): chags pa che la gcig tu dga'//yul gyi phrag dog rnam par spangs//bag yod rtag tu dga' ba dang//rnal 'byor ma yin snying po skyong//rdo rje theg par cig tu mos//theg pa dman la rgyab kyis 
7b5(1): phyogs|de la sogs pa'i yon tan ldan//bla ma slob mas bsnyen bkur bya//yang dag bla ma mnyes nas ni//slob ma'i rang bzhin dri ma rnams//dag dang rtogs dang ston byas nas//
7b6(1): ji bzhin ye shes bzang dang ldan//ji ltar rin chen bdar ba dang//gser ni sreg dang bcad pas brtag/de bzhin du ni slob ma yang//de phyir phan tshun de lta bus//dus kun du ni de ltar brtag/
7b7(1): gzhan du bgegs ni 'byung gyur te//dngos grub 'joms shing gdugs pa ster//dug ni sngags kyis bcom pa bzhin//sngags kyis dag byas bcud len byed//de bzhin slob ma bdabang  
8a1(1): @#//sogs kyis//ma dag pa ni dag par byed//de phyir 'bad pa thams cad kyis//sngags pas slob ma dag byas nas//bla mas dbang bskur la sogs kyis//
8a2(1): dngos grub phyir ni ji ltar ston//de phyir slob ma dbang sogs kyis//ma dag pa yang dag par 'gyur//rdo rje sems dpa' bde chen la//de nas la gan rdo rjes zhus//dbang bskur ji ltar rgyud du mi/
8a3(1): gtso bo ru ni gsungs pa lags//rdo rje sems dpa' thams cad mkhyen//the tsom thams cad gtso bo mdzad pa//de kho na nyid mkhyen pa ni//lag na (de)rje la gsungs pa yin//don de ji 
8a4(1): lta ba bzhin du//nga yis yang dag bshad kyis nyon//dbang bskur ba yi dbye ba ni//dang po dbang ni gtso bo ste//gnyis pa gsang ba'i ming can no//gsum pa kun nas sbyor ba ste//
8a5(1): bzhi pa don ni dam pa'o//gtso bo dbye ba bcu gcig ste//bcu gnyis pa ni gsang ba po//bcu gsum pa ni yang dag sbyor//bcu bzhi pa don dam ste//dbang bskur re res sa re'o//
8a6(1): rnam pa gsum ni rgyu'i dbang//bzhi(pa) 'bras bur shes par bya//dga' ba sogs rim pa nyid//thams cad la ni nga yis khyab//yar ngo la ni tshes gsum dang//de bzhin tshes bcu 
8a7(1): tshes bcu bzhi//yon tan bzang ldan slob ma la//bla mas chog bzhin dbang bskur//lus 'bra lo ni bcu drug lon//gzugs dang skal ba zang dag gis rgyan//sngo sangs rgyas 
8b1(1): mig ni yangs pa dang//snying rje bsam ldan yang dag mos//ngag snyan dbang bskur thob pa dang//rnal 'byor rgyud la lhag par mos//yang na lo ni bcu gnyis lon//nyams 
8b2(1): dga' dben par byed pa ste//de las lhag par skal med 'gyur//de ni dngos grub ring du byed//lhag par yid 'ong bu mo 'am//chung ma 'am yang na sring mo 'am//yang na 
8b3(1): bu mo dad pa yis//bla ma la ni dbul bar//gser dang rin chen gos dang ni//mu tig phreng ba sna tshogs pas//mchod nas bla ma'i mdun gus te//slob ma y+si ni 'di skad brjod 
8b4(1): de nas brtsams te bra dag bran du//gtso bos bdag ni mchod par bzhes//de lta'i rnam pa mnyes nas ni//bdag nyid kun gyis bla ma mchog/bla ma'i ngo bo rdo rje 'chang//de phyir yang 
8b5(1): dag bla ma mchod//de nas bcom ldan 'das yum phyag rgya//yang dag mchog nas sten par bya//sten pa las ni gang byung ba//ltung bskal bzangs kha la ni//sbom po mtha' lhag sbyor ba 
8b6(1): yis//yang dag par ni longs spyad bya//de las skyes pa'i ye shes ni//bde ba chen po rang rig pa//de nas bla ma dam pa yis//slob ma pa de la phyag rgya gtang//srog dang rtsol ba mnyam 
8b7(1): sbyor bas//byang chub sems ni bzung bar bya//hUM phaT sngags ni nor bu'i sgor//bkod de rdo rje rab bskyod bya//dga' ba rnams ji ltar ba bzhin du//skad cig de las skyes 'gyur bas//
9a1(1): @#//bla ma dam pas ngag sems kyis//rdo rje slob mas de la brjod de nas slob ma'i lag tu ni//phyag rgya gtad nas 'di skad brjod//rdo rje btsun mo'i kha nas brjod//
9a2(1): de yang slob ma'i lag tu ni//ji ltar pad mchod bya ba ni//byos la e boM mchog ni 'di//gang phyir rdo rje dbyings dbang phyug/rang nyid ni 'di ni snying la bzhugs//thabs gzhan gyis ni    
9a3(1): sangs rgyas nyid//rnal 'byor ba yis tho ba mi 'gyur//de'i rnam pa shin tu phra//bde chen rnal 'byor rtogs pa yis//rab tu' bad pa sten par gyis//lhan cig skyes dga'i mtshan nyid ni//
9a4(1): dbang bskur gtso bor gyur pas na//thams cad ji ltar ba bzhin thob//rnal 'byor chen po'i rgyud//dpal rdo rje 'phreng ba mngon par rtogs pa la//slob dpon dang slob ma brtag pa dang//dbang 
9a5(1): bskur ba'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas de nyid gzhan yang nyon//rdo rje 'phreng ba don gsal ba//rgyud rnams kun tu spas pa ni//gang yang rung la brjod mi bya//rdo rje 'phreng 
9a6(1): bzhes bya'i ming//de yang sa ni bcu gsum pa//dga' ba bzhi ni don gyi gnas//thams cad stong pa la rten pa//gang gi spyod yul kun las 'das//gnyis pa thams cad rnam par  
9a7(1): spangs//skal bzangs rnal 'byor kun sogs kyi//rnal 'byor ma yin ngag gi yul//stong nyid ye shes rdo rje dang//rdo rje 'phreng bzhes bshad do//stong nyid rdo rje gsang pad+ma//rtsa ni rdor dbyings 
9b1(1): dbang phyug mchog/ye shes mchog tu bde ba che//lhan cig skyes dga' mtshan nyid pa//rdo rje de yang rnam rtog rnams//dmigs pa med par yongs su grags//'phreng ba de rnams kyi ni yang rgyun//
9b2(1): byang chub sems kyi rdo rje rnams//mi 'byed par ni rdo rjer gsungs//rgyud rnams kun tu sbas pa ni//gang yang rung bar brjod mi bya//dmigs pa med yongs su grags//'phrengab rgyun ni mi 'chad pa//
9b3(1): dga' ba('a)ba ba bdag nyid kyis//rnam rtag rlung kun tu 'bigs par mi nus so//nyin dang mtshan mo rtag tu ni//brgya dang brgyad lhag gsal ba yang //rnam rtog dang bzhin rlung rnams ni//
9b4(1): 'tshar ba dang ni 'joms pa can/ji lta bar ni rab byed bye ba'i//rlung rnams kyi ni ming nyin cig/btang bzung dang ni dus 'phur dang //myos dang ni blo dman/dang //gsil byed dang 
9b5(1): ni g.ya' ba dang //dar snabs dang ni rta re dang //zegs pa chu srin dza sha dang //rnam par brtsigs dang myong ba'i dus//'dod myos chu ba chu srin dang //sna tshogs skyes dang sdug 
9b6(1): pa dang //snyems dang rngam dang sgra sgrogs dang //rlung la dang ni rnam 'bigs dang //me dang dus na ci bgyi dang //rdo rje mchog sbyin cu ta dang //sprang ma dang ni zla ba'i mthong //
9b7(1): kam por dza ral pa can//yungs na ga ra ba bkram dus krugs dang //sdig pa dang ni g.yo can ston//grogs med ni dang ni mgrin pa dang //kun sel mchog ste myos byed dang//
10a1(1): @#/ O /dag gi bri ba ste/yang na mtha' lta gakhrag dang ni/nim pa'i lo ma'i khu ba dang /ras dmar po'i khu ba dang/ci rnams bsres te bris bar bya/spri 
10a2(1): tshal gyis ni 'khor mo bri/bum pa mar dang ldan par bzhag/dus bzhi dag tu mchod pa ni/me tog dmar pos mchod par bya/de tshe bzlas pa'i tshul dag ni/thod pa kha sbyar nang bu
10a3(1): bzhag|bzlas ba khris ni brgya byin gyis/bu mo 'di 'ang brnyed par nges 'gyur ro /dur khrod kyis                 ni ras dag la/gur gum gyis ni 'khor lo bris/yi ge thig le rnams
10a4(1): bskor te/a mu kyi dang a mu kyi/mo ha na dang rnam spel la/bsgrub bya gos kyi tha ma la/legs gtags chos kyi dbyings nyid du /bsgoms nas bzlos shing 'gron 
10a5(1): mang;lha dang lha min gyis mi mthong /bdag nyid gshin rje nyid bsgoms nas/chu'i steng du 'gro 'dod na/rdul phran lha'i rnam par ste//dbu rgyan du ni bdag rang 
10a6(1): nyid /nges par steng du gro gzhan dag kyang /bu mo'i nags kyi ras pal la/skud pa bkal ba gzhon nu mas/gsum bsgril du yang de yis bya/gdug sngags stong du mtshad 
10a7(1): ma ste/bzlas tshul sngon du gsungs ba bzhin//skar ma rgyal gyis dus la bzlas//bri ba'i nyin mo 'ang la'o//dkyil 'khor gyis ni lhar btag pa'o//g.yon gyi mdud byas 
10b1(1): bzhan la btags/ma rungs mtshon dgarengs par byed/me tog las kyang gzhan du min /sha lnga la sogs ril bu ni /legs par grib ma dag la skam/lcags 
10b2(1): gsum dag gyis dkris par bya/bzlas shing mchod pa byed pa'i tshe/rdul dag bdag rang bzhin bya/'bar bar gyur pa nyid na yang /mchod pa legs pa rnams byaste//
10b3(1): bskor ba brgya phrag du ma bya/de nas blo ldan bsgrub pa pos/khar bcug tsam gyis 'dod pa bsgrub/phyung nas sngon bltas rnal 'byor nyid /gso' ba dran pa'o mas kyang //
10b4(1): nges par 'khrul 'khor kru bar bya/rigs sngags 'khor las grol te/rnal ma nyid la gnas pa yin//snum pa de dag gis bon la/nya lcibs dang ni byi thang ka'i/khu ba'i 
10b5(1): yang yang de byugs la/thod pa pas ni rnam bkang bar /sbas te bzlas pa drangs med byas/de tshe chod dang gtor ma dang /yongs rgyas 'bras bu rnams blangs te/khar 
10b6(1): bcug mkha' 'gro byar 'gyur ro /de la 'di skad ces bya ba ste/zhus dang 'das dus 'byung ba'i zhing /mngon par rtogs pa'i ting nge 'dzin/dkyil 'khor rnams par baspos pa 
10b7(1): thig gi gzhal yas khang mtshon dang /rdul mtshon phyogs las rnam par brtag/yang dag byin gyis brlabs pa dang /bdud ci myang bar bya ba dang /mtshangs pa'i gnas bzhi bsgo 
11a1(1): ka @#// O //bzhin med//bem po log par nga rgyal dang//'gyod dang 'khrel yod ba dang//gus pa med dang ches pa med//mthong man ngo mtsha med dang mchi//tshul chos ngo 
11a2(1): tsha shes dang ngo tsha med//mi ster ba dang mya ngan dang//yid gnyis dang ni rloms sems med//sgribs dang the tsom ngal ba dang//dbang dang 'khrul ba snying stobs dang//snyams sems pa dang  
11a3(1): rgyal ba dang//'thim pa dang ni mi srun pa gya gyur smin dang myos pa dang//rigs byed min dang ba ga yod dang//gnas ngan len dang nyams chung nyid//yid kyi bde ba ltag par rtogs//
11a4(1): sgrag dang rmugs dang gnyin dang ni//brkam chags rim pa khrung khrung dang//'tshe ba le log yon po nyid//bsam spyod pa dang zhum pa dang//kun rdzob de nyid don dman dang//ster dang dga' 
11a5(1): ba'i dang pa dang//'jig+sa rten dang 'da' dang pra bdag nyid//'brel dang rtag pa'i de nyid dang//rus sbal dom med gnod pa dang//mi gsal ba dang yid du 'ong//gnas med par du smra 
11a6(1): ba dang ࿒ bdag nyid skyes phud nyid dang ni//ya mtshan dga' dga' skye bo ngan//dman pa'i de nyid sdug bsngal gsum//'khor ba la ni chags pa dang//rtag tu brgya dang brgyad lhag ma//
11a7(1): nyin dang mtshan mo rtag tu ni//rlung gi rang bzhin 'gyur ba yin//rnal 'byor chen mo'i rgyud//dpal rdo rje 'phreng ba mngon par rtogs pa las//rdo rje 'phreng ba'i tshigs gi dbye ba ste//
11b1(1): le'u gsum pa'o////de nas rdo rje gsum nyi mas//gsang ba'i pad+ma kha 'byed pa//bde ba chen po'i go 'phang bzhugs//'dus pa'i mchog don rab gsungs pa//'dus pa 'dres pa 
11b2(1): zhes ni gsungs//'dres pa nga yis khyod la bshad//'khor lo bzhi rnams tha mi dad//sngags ni lhan cig cho ga bzhin//dpa' bo'i rnal 'byor nga sbyor ba//gang tshe gsang ba'i rtsa gnyis 
11b3(1): ni|/yang dag sbyor bas reg 'gyur ba//de tshe nam mkha' rnam rgyas nyid//nam mkha' las ni skyes pa rlung//de yi me ni rab tu 'bar//rtsa ni stong phrag bdun cu gnyis//rtsa rnams rab 
11b4(1): bsad gzi brjid kyis//bde ba kun gyi go 'phang mchog/bde ba chen po'i 'khor los khyab//bde ba chen po'i byang chub sems//rnam par zhu nas rtsar gnas pa//rdor gsum rang 
11b5(1): bzhin cig 'gyur phyir//khams rnams ro cig gyur pa dang//phung po rnams ni yongs zad dang//ngal so ba la rlung se rnams ni//dga' rnams la ni yang dag skye//de nas byang chub sem+sa 
11b6(1): kyang ni//gnas kun nas ni zhu gyur nas//ngo bo gcig tu gyur pa dang//rtsa ni cig gis 'bras bu yi//ro gcig tu ni gyur pa la//'dres pa zhes ni de la gsungs//phung po khams dang  
11b7(1): dbang po dang//yul rnams dang ni spas pa dang//rnam rtog kun nas zad pa las//yang dag ye shes bde chen po//rdo rje gsum ni ro cig pa//'dus pa zhes ni bya bar brjod//de kho ni  
12a1(1): @#//na nyid mchog tu gsungs//rdo rje sems dpa'i go 'phang gsal//rnal 'byor chen po'i rgyud//dpal rdo rje 'phreng ba mngon par rtogs pa las/'das pa'i tshig lhag par bshad pa'i/
12a2(1): le'u ste bzhi pa'o////de nas gzhan yang de nyid don//stong nyid don ni rab rgyas pa//stong pa nyid kyi ni chos kun gyi//nang du khyab pa 'ang chos nyid do//de nyid yang dag 
12a3(1): shes nas ni//de bzhin nyid dang rnam mi rtog/bdag dang gang zag chos rnams ni//bdag med rang bzhin gcig pa gnyid//stong pa nyid dang rang rig pa//thams cad bdag nyid rtag zhugs pa//
12a4(1): rnal 'byor cig bu'i rtogs pa ste//rtog gengs pa yi spyod yul min//chos rnams thams cad mngon bsdus nas//'khor los drug tu rnam par brjod//de dag nang tu rtag khyab pa//
12a5(1): rtsa ba'i 'khor los legs rgyas pa/brjod //de ni nam mkha' khyab cing 'phro//khyab byed zhi ba'i mtshan nyid can//dmigs pa med cing rten pa med//gzugs med nyon mongs med pa dang//
12a6(1): rnam par mi rtog mchog tu zhi//rang bzhin med cing dri ma med//gnyis med rten pa bya ba med gzung dang 'dzin pa rnam pa spangs//dus dang dpel 'das (pa)ste//ngag gi las ni spyod yul bral//
12a7(1): chos nyid chos (ni)thams cad kyi//ji lta ba bzhin yang dag brjod//dngos dang dngos med rnam par bral//dge dang mi dge srid las grol//de ni kun nas sgrib pa med//nam mkha' lta bur mchog 
12b1(1): tu khyab//chos rnams ji lta ba bzhin skye//de ni de bzhin ces brjod//gang nas kyang ni ma 'ongs zhing//gang du yang ni 'gro ba med//mongs pa 'khrul pa cig tu las//de ni rab tu spros ba 
12b2(1): par snang//bde ba dang ni sdug bsngal spangs//'dod chags zhe sdang sogs mi dgos//'khrul dang rnam par 'khrul las bral//rtog pa dang ni dri ma med//bdag dang srogs dang bskyed pa po//
12b3(1): skye bu de bzhin gang zag dang//sheng las skyes dang shed bu dang//byed pa po dang tshor ba po//shes pa po dang mthong ba po//sgro 'dogs pa ni rnam par spangs//phung po skye mched khams la sogs//
12b4(1): rnam shes dang ni gzung ba yis//sgrib pa med cing mchog tu phra//nam mkha' mchog la yang dag gnas//de ltar ma lus stong nyid don//brjod dang brjod bya'i don dang bral//
12b5(1): ming tsam cig bu kho na yang//rang 'byung mchog tu rmad 'byung ba//brjod pa yi ni kun rdzob du//de ni nga yis yang dag bshad//rang rig shin tu phra pa'i phyir//de kho na nyid mthong ba med//
12b6(1): bdag nyid rang gi ngo bo rig//pha rol gzhan gyi spyod yul min//de ni rang gi rig par mi bshad//chos kun gnas ni zhi ba'o//rnam rtog gi 'khrul rtog ge ba//mi shes pa yi mongs pa dang//
12b7(1): don dam pa ni mi shes shing//kun rdzob sgra yis brjod 'dzin pa//ye shes mchog ni mi shes shing//rtog ge stong par smra bar byed//rnal 'byor bsam gtan rnal 'byor pa//
15a1(1): @#//kyis brtags pa'i ting nge 'dzin//byang chub sbyor ba bsgom par bya//de nas dpal ldan rdo rje 'dzin//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyis mchod/sangs rgyas kun mchog kun 
15a2(1): mkhyen gyis//bsgom pa'i mchog ni bka' stsal to//nam ba'i dbyings kyis dbus gnas par//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor bsgom par bya//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rab bsgom(sa) par nas//phra mo'i sbyor 
15a3(1): ba kun tu brtsam//sna yi rtse mo yungs kar bsam//'gro dang mi 'gro yungs kar la//ye shes gnas ni nyams dga' ba//gsang ba'i ye shes kyis ngag bsgom//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi 
15a4(1): dbus gnas par//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor bsgom par ( )bya//sangs rgyas sku yi gnas sgom te//de'i steng du gnas bzhag go//namakha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//gsal pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi 
15a5(1): bsgom par bya//spyan gyi tshul du sbyor ba la//rdo rje pad+mo rnam par bsgom//nam mkha'i dbying kyi dbus gnas par//rin chen dkyil 'khor bsgom par bya //mchog gi sbyor ba nan tan gyis//
15a6(1): de yi steng du yongs su sgom//namakha'i dbyings kyi dbu gnas(sa)//pad+ma'i dkyil 'khor bsgom par  bya//gsung gi rdo rje tshul sbyor bas//'dod chags rdo rje can bsgom bya//namakha'i dbyings 
15a7(1): kyi dbus kyi dbus gnas par/'od kyi dkyil 'khor bsgom par bye//sangs rgyas tshul ni zhi ba spro//ut+pal mthing ka'i mdog 'dra ba//rtse mo lnga pa khyad par du//gnas kyi 'bru tsam nan tan gyis//
15b1(1): sna yi rtse mor rnam par bsam//tsa na ka yi 'bru tsam la//'dab ma brgyad pa gser bcas//sna yi rtse mor 'di shis te//bsgom pas byang chub gzhol bar 'gyur//'khor lo la  
15b2(1): sogs khyad par du//bsgom pa de'i ni brtag par bya//dngos grub thams cad yon tan gnas//nyams dga' byang chub gnas 'grub 'gyur//sangs rgyas byang chub gnas rnams ni//
15b3(1): de ru mdor na spro bar bya//sku gsung thugs kyis mtshan ma yis//chos kyi tshig rnams rab tu brjod//de nas dpal ldan rdo rje 'dzin//de nyid don kun ston pa pos//spyod pa kun mchog leg+sa
15b4(1): dag pas//gsang ba'i dam pa bka' stsal to//gzugs dang sgra dang ror ldan pa//shes rab can gyi zla drug bsgom//gsang ba'i de nyid mchod chen bsgom par gyis//legs par mchod nas 
15b5(1): bsgom par bya'o//dngos grub 'brus bu 'dod pa yis//zas su bshang gci zos na ni//sangs rgyas byang chub skyon med pa//yang dag bla na med grub par 'gyur//zas su sha    
15b6(1): rnams za ba dang//sha chen dag ni ra ba brtag+sa na//sku dang gsung thugs gsang ba yi//dngos grub thams cad 'grub bar 'gyur//glang po'i sha dang rta'i sha//de bzhin dam pa khyi yi 
15b7(1): sha/zas kyi phyir ni bza' bar bya//zas gzhan dag ni ma zos na//byang chub sems dpa' blo can dang//sangs rgyas rnams ni mnyes par 'gyur//'di lta bu yi sbyor ba yin//
17a1(1): ka @#//rtogs dang rtogs pa rnam par grol//skye med zag med rtag tu 'byung //'dzin dang mngon zhen la sogs spangs//spangs shig lhag ma med pa dang//
17a2(1): mchog ni mchog ste zhi ba ni//dngos kun gyis dbus na gnas//'jug pa'i rnam shes tshogs drug gi//khyab byed du yang yang dag gnas//rnam rtog med cing brten pa med//
17a3(1): kun gzhi'i rnam shes shes zhes gsungs so//nyon mongs can gyis yid kyis ni//rnam rtog med gnyid du gnas//bem po nyid ni sang par byed//mchog tu gnyis med rnal 'byor gyis/
17a4(1): thob pa'i go 'phang bla na med//rtog med 'jig med gsal ba dang//rlung gi de nyid yang dag gnas//yi ge ho yis bskul nas ni//me yi 'khor lo rabasad de//de ltar nA da'i go 'phang don//
17a5(1): bla ma'i zhal las yang dag gnas//rnal 'byor chen po'i rgyud//dpal rdo rje 'phreng ba mngon par rtogs pa las//nA da'i tshig rnam par bshad pa'i le'u ste bdun pa'o////
17a6(1): de nas gzhan yang gsang ba'i don//bla na med pa bshad kyi nyon//gsang ba'i b+ha ga zhes ni gsungs//b+ha ga de bzhin chos 'byung//bde ba chen po'i go 'phang rten//dga' ba skyes 
17a7(1): par byed pa'i mchog /rdo rje sems dpa' yi ni gnas//nam mkha' lnga'i bdag nyid gsol//ye shes chen po spros pa med//rnam par rtog pa thams cad spangs//bral dga' 
17b1(1): yi ni mtha' ste mchog/lhan cig skyes bka' kun bdag gnas//dga' bral la sogs skad cig 'das//'phra ba yang dag byang chub sems//lus la phan par 'tsho ba sten//
17b2(1): srang mngon 'khrul 'khor lta bur gnas//rtog med rtog pa'i yul las 'das//stong nyid skye bzang pa med//sprul pa'i 'khrul lor gnas pa dang //bde ba chen po'i dkyil 'khor 
17b3(1): skye/bsam gyis mi khyab khyab zhi ba//chags bral kun 'gro skye ba med//rdo rje pad+ma mnyam sbyor bas//rtogs pa bde ba thams cad ster//rlung gi de nyid rig pa yis//rdo rje dbus su 'dzin par 
17b4(1): byed//gang tshe skye ba nyams myong bar//gsang ba zhes ni bya bar grags//de nyid bsang ba yi ni mchog/bla ma'i zhal la lhag par gnas//phan chun rgyud pa'i rim pa yis//bla ma bzang 
17b5(1): po'i zhal las shes//rnal 'byor/chen po'i rgyud//dpal rdo rje 'phreng ba mngon par rtogs las//gsang ba chen po'i tshogs ma lus pa ston pa zhes bya ba'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o////
17b6(1): de nas gzhan las yang sdom pa'i don//de kho na nyid bshad kyis nyon//sdom pa bsrung ba byed pa gang //de ni byang chub sems zhes brjod//rdo rje rtse mo sems 'dzin pa//de ni bsrung ba 
17b7(1): yongs su grags//dbang po rnams ni yul rnams las//'jug cing gang du gang gnas pa//de ni sdom pa bsrung ba dang //dam tshigs bsrung bar bshad pa yin//gdul bka' tshul 
30a1(1): @#//ltas dbyug pa dang//dbyug snyems dang ni snying po dang//tshigs kyi lto dang tshigs kyi tshig/tshigs kyi tshad dang rngub pa dang//tshigs kyi gnas 
30a2(1): dang tshigs kyis gdon//tshigs 'od tshigs kyi lu gu rgyud//dul byed dul 'gro gzi brjod dang//dri med dul ba'i snyems ma dang//chun 'phyang lto dang chun 'phyang chag+sa//
30a3(1): chun 'chang chun 'phyang bye brag tu//phru gu bu ster 'gro ba dang//lo ma lo ma'i mgrin pa dang//rab mchog ma dang ra ba gsal te//de ltar drug cu rtsa bzhir brjod//lte ba sprul  
30a4(1): pa'i 'khor lor gnas//bdud rtsi mchog gis yongs su gang//rin chen cod pan pad+ma che//rdo rje chen po stobs po che//mgo bo che dang sgra chen dang//nyer dga' chen po 
30a5(1): myos pa che//'di brgyad chos kyi 'khor lo'i dbus//snying kha'i pad+mar yang dag gnas//longs spyod longs spyod ster dang gsang//nyin 'gro gsal dang skal pa dang/
30a6(1): bsgom pa dang ni thal ba'i gtong//bza' ster bza' dang skres pa dang//bcud len ro ni sgyur dang//ro 'jig pa dang ro dang ni//khrung khrung longs spyod 'khor lor gnas//
30a7(1): grangs ni bcu drug du ni brjod//de ste rim rigs 'dod pa can//'dod pa can dang srog gi lus//zla gdong zla ster mdzes dang ni//'dod can mi smra kun sha dang//shi'i ba'i spong  
30b1(1): dang dug pa dang//me dang mi ro ne rtso dang//gang pa nyid dang ro ldan pa//kos rings chang ster bdud rtsi dang//dung ban ni dung na dang//dung gi mig dang dung gi sna//rgyags dbang rgyags la 
30b2(1): bskyings pa dang//rgyags pa rgyal dang ngo mtshar rgyags//snyems pa dang ni tshogs kyi rten//sum cu rtsa gnyis zhes ni bshad//bde ba chen po'i 'khor lo che//mchog tu dga' 
30b3(1): bster bar byed//'phra dang reg bya mchog tu phra//gzung dang 'dzin pa rnams par spangs//rang nyid bde chen rang bzhin dang//gzhan dag la yang bsten par byed//de ltar yang dag 
30b4(1): rtsa yi rim//bla ma'i zhal las shes par bya//rtsa yi rim de nges pa ni//phung po la sogs mi 'byung zhing//phung po la sogs med na ye shes che//rnal 'byor pa yis thob mi 'gyur//
30b5(1): phung po lnga ni bdag nyid lus//mkha' 'gro lngar ni rab tu grags//de nyid 'byung ba lnga yi ni//ye shes lngar ni rnam par gnas//de phyir 'bad pa thams cad kyis//rtsa yi 'khor 
30b6(1): lo shes par bya//ji ltar chu'i dbus kyi shing//myur du yang ni 'phel nas 'gro//de las 'bras bu sogs ldan pa//ster ba 'di la the tshom med//de bzhin phung po shing zhe  
30b7(1): bya|rtsa chu yi ni 'phel nas gang che rdo rje pad+ma sbyor//de tshe me ni yang dga' bar//rnga 'phel ba yang dag 'bras bu ni//thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ster bya//lha yi rnam pa kho ni 
16a1(2): @#//myur du sangs rgyas nyid thob 'gyur//'dod khams dbang phyug 'jig rten na//go 'phang las ni byed par 'gyur//gtso bo gze can stobs dang ldan//mdangs bzang 
16a2(2): mthong na yid du 'ong//mthong ba tsam gyis bskul med par//'jig rten 'di dag dbang du 'gyur//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyis//gsang ba byang chub dam pa ste//yang dag sngags kyi 
16a3(2): gsang ba 'di//lus ngag sems las 'da' ba'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi gsang ba gsang ba 'dus pa las/sku dang gsung dang thugs kyis byin gyis 
16a4(2): rlob pa'i le'u ste drug pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung thugs kyi bdag pos//dam tshig chen po'i de kho na nyid kyi sngags 
16a5(2): kyi spyod pa'i mchog mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa'i le'u 'di gsungs so//'dod pa'i longs spyod thams cad ni//ji ltar 'dod par bsten na ni//'di lta bu yi sbyor pa yis//sangs rgyas 
16a6(2): myur du thob par 'gyur//'dod pa'i longs spyod thams cad ni//ji ltar 'dod par bsten bya ste//rang gi lha yi sbyor ba yis//bdag dang gzhan la mchod par bya//dka' thub sdom pa mi bzad 
16a7(2): pas//bsten kyang 'grub par mi 'gyur gyi//'dod pa'i longs spyod mthas cad la//bsten na myur du 'grub par 'gyur//slongs mo za zhing mi bzla ste/slong la'ang dga' bar mi bya'o//'dod pa 
16b1(2): kun la longs spyod cing/sngags kyi yan lag mnyams bzlas//lus dang ngag dang sems rnams ni//bde bar gnas na byang chub thob//gzhan du dus min 'chi ba dang//dmyal bar sreg 
16b2(2): par 'gyur//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' dang//sngags kyi spyod pa mchog spyod rnams//chos mchog mi g.yo brnyes pa yang//'dod pa thams cad bsten pas so//chags can ye shes 
16b3(2): 'dod pa yis//rtag tu 'dod yon lnga rnams bsten//byang chub sems dpa' mnyes par bya//byang chub nyi ma chags par bya//gzugs la rnam gsum shes byas nas//mchod par byed pas 
16b4(2): mchod par bya//de nyid bcom ldan sna tshogs pa//sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad gzhi//sgra la rnam gsum shes byas na//lha rnams la ni dbul bar bya//de nyid bcom ldanasna tshogs pa//
16b5(2): sangs rgyas rin chen 'byung gnas gtso//dri la rnam gsum shes byas na sa/sangs rgyas sogs la dbul bar bya//de nyidabacom ldan sna tshogs pa/'dod chags kyi 'byung gnas gtso//
16b6(2): ro la rnam gsum shes byas na'//bdag gi rigs la dbul bar bya//de nyid bcom ldan rdo rje can//mi bskyod pa yi tshul brnyes pa'o//gzugs sgra rol sogs pa la//rtag tu sems ni spyad 
16b7(2): par bya//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//gsang ba'i snying po btus pa'o//gzugs sgra dri sogs sngags pa yis//lha nyid du ni rtag bsgom//yang na de la bsgom pa ni//rigs kyi tshul 
19a1(2): @#//bshad//na chung gzhon nu mi Ig.yangs pa//gzugs bzang gzhon pas brgyan pa ni//lo grangs nyi shu lnga lon rnyed//byol song las kyang rab tu brtag/sa phyogs gtsang zhing rnam par 
19a2(2): dben pa'am//rgyal nyid skyes zhi zhi ba'i gnas rnams su//bshang gci chu la sogs pas byug pa'am//rgyal ba mchod phyir rtag par btu bya//nu ma'i dbus par spyi gtsug mtha'i bar//cho ga shes pas yang na  
19a3(2): rkang ba'i bar//lte brked pa gsang bar rgyal ba'i sras//rigs lnga rnams ni dgod par rab tu bya//namakha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//ye shes rgya mtsho dus bsgom par bya//bdag nyid zla ba'i dbus gan+sa 
19a4(2): par//mkhas pas snying khar bsgom par bya//zhi ba rdo rje 'dzin 'dod na//bsdu ba dag kyang rab tu bya//rin chen po sna bzhi'i mchod rten la//'od gzer 'phreng bas rnam par brgyan//ye shes rgya mtsho bsgom  
19a5(2): dus gsum gyi//gnas de yin par rnam par bsam//bdag gi ba spu'i bu gnas//mkhas pas mchod pa'i sprin rnams spro//mkhas pa u t+pa la dag dang//pad+ma rnam pa lngar shes bya//dzA ti rnam gsum sh+se 
19a6(2): byas nas//lha rnams la ni dbul bar bya//me tog ka Ni kA dag dang//ma li kA dang yU thi ka/me tog ka ra bi ra bsam//mchod pa thams cad brtag/dpag tshad brgya yi khyon tsam du//'khor lo'i 
19a7(2): dkyil 'khor bsgom byas la//rnam par mkhas pas rtag par ni//rigs rnams rab tu dgod par bya//de bzhin pad+mo rdo rje dang//ral gri ut+pal mkhas pas bsgom//dpag tshad bye ba'i khyon tsam du//
19b1(2): gru bzhi shin tu mdzes pa yi//mchod rten rin chen bzhi  pa//gsal zhing rang bzhin dri ma med//rigs rnams thams cad mchod pa'i phyir//shes rab can gyi gnas su bsgom//'dod pa'i yon tan lnga rnam+sa 
19b2(2): kyis //de la mchod pa rgya mtsho brtsam//rin chen na bzang la sogs pas//byang chub 'dod pas rtag tu mchod//mchod pa rnam lnga mchog rnams kyis//rtag tu lha rnams mnyes par bya//rin chen 'byung   
19b3(2): gnas na chung mchog/rin chen sna tshogs mchog gis brgyan//sgrub tsher ba tu 'grub pa'i phyir//sangs rgyas kun la dbul bar bya//zhing 'di rin chen sna bdun gyis//mkhas pa dag gis yongs bkang la//
19b4(2): dngos grub 'dod pas sbyin pa'i phyir//nyin re shes rab can gyis dbul//rgyal po'i phyag rgyar mnyam bzhag la//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor dbus dag tu//'dod chags blo can sangs rgyas la//reg pa'i sbyor ba 
19b5(2): dbul bar bya//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//dkyil 'khor bkod ba bsgom par bya//de bzhin gshegs pa bdag gis lus//bla ma bshad dang gci bas mchod//bzhin bzang shin 
19b6(2): tu mdzes pa yis//na chung skal ba mchog ldan pa//byin gyis brlabs kyi gnas bsam te//sangs rgyas mchod pa rab tu brtag /mig bzang brtan pa'i blo ldan pas//khu ba blangs nas bza'  
19b7(2): bar bya//'di ni sngags rnams thams cad kyi//sku gsung thugs kyi mchod pa ste//ye shes rdo rje can gyi gsang//sngags rnams dngos grub byed par gsungs//de bzhin gshegs pa
29a1(2): @#//pad+ma chen po 'dab brgyad la//rang sngags rdo rje bsams nas su//chos rnams kun gyi gnas yin par//bsams na chos dang 'dra bar 'gyur//rir bsum cu rtsa drug gi/rdul phran    
29a2(2): ji snyed yod pa yi//sangs rgyas chos mchog dkyil 'khor du//dag pa'i bdag nyid 'jogs par byed//pad+mo mnyam pa nyid ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//ye shes lnga ldan rab bsten ni//bskal pa   
29a3(2): gsum du yun du gnas//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//gsang ba gsum la bsnyen bkur byed//'od gzer lnga yi mdog 'dra'i 'od//rang gi sngags kyi ral gri bsgom//mig yangs 
29a4(2): lag na thog na ni//rdo rje gsum rig 'dzin par 'gyur//khams gsum kun gyis rab mchod pa cing//tshangs dbang lha ni na phyag byas pa//stong gsum na ni gcig bu pa//mi mchog gsang ba 'dzin par 'gyur/
29a5(2): gang rnams 'dod dang bsam pa dag//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje 'dzin//rdo rje'i thugs las rab tu byung//de lta bu yi dngos grub ster//ral gi thams cad kyi dam pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin te//oM ni ri 
29a6(2): lu tsa na ka'i//'bru tshad tsam du bsams nas ni//'brus bu rang gi lha yi gzugs//bsams te khar bcug rnam bsgoms na//de ni de ma tha ga tu yang//byang chub sems dpa'i 'od 'drar 'gyur//
29a7(2): nyi ma shar ba'i 'od 'dra zhing//dzam+bu chu bo'i gzer gyi mdog/AHni ri lu tsa na ka'i//'bru tshang tsam du bsams nas ni//'bras su rang gi lha yi gzugs//bsams te khar bcug rnamabasgoms na//de ni 
29b1(2): de na ma thag tu yang //byang chub ye shes 'od 'drar 'gyur//nyi ma shar ba'i 'od 'dra zhing //dzAm+bu chu bo'i gser gyi mdog/hUM ni rilu tsan ka'i//'bru tshad tsam du bsams nas ni//dbus su rang gi 
29b2(2): lha yi gzugs//bsams te khar bcug rnam bsgoms na//de ni de ma thag tu yang //rdo rje'i sku dang 'od 'drar 'gyur//mkha' dbyings gsal ba'i dbus gnas par//rnam par snang mdzad rab tu 
29b3(2): bsgom//lag tu 'khor lo bsgoms na//'khor lo'i rig pa 'dzin par 'gyur//'khor lo chen po'i rigs bsgoms te//sangs rgyas skur ni rab sbyor zhing //ye shes 'di ni rab sgrub pa//ye shes 
29b4(2): rdo rje dag gis bya//mkha' dbyings rdo rje'i ye shes dbus gnas par//ye shes mi bskyod rab bsgom te//lag tu rdo rje rab bsgoms na//rdo rje'i rig pa 'dzin par 'gyur //rdo rje chen po'i rigs bsgom+sa 
29b5(2): te|rdo rje sku ni rab sbyor zhing //rdo rje'i mchog 'di sgrub pa ni//ye shes rdo rje dag gis bya//mkha'ang dbyings rin chen dbus gnas par//rin chen rdo rje rab tu bsgom//lag tu rin chen rab bsgoms na//
29b6(2): rin chen rig pa 'dzin par 'gyur//rin po che yi rigs bsgoms nas//rin chen skur ni rab sbyor zhing //rin chen mchog 'di sgrub pa ni//rdo rje ye shes dag gis bya//mkha' dbyibs chos kyi dbus gnas 
29b7(2): par//tshe dpag tu med pa rab tu bsgom//lag tu pad+mo rab bsgoms na//pad+mo'i rig pa 'dzin par 'gyur//pad+mo'i rigs chen bsgoms nas su//chos kyi skur ni rab sbyor zhing //pad+ma'i mchog 'di sgrub 
30a1(2): @#//pa ni//ye shes rdo rje dag gis bya//mkha' dbyings dam tshig dbus gnas par//gdon mi za ba'i ye shes bsgom//lag tu ral gri rab bsgoms na//ral gri'i rig pa 'dzin par   
30a2(2): 'gyur//dam tshig chen po'i rigs bsgoms nas//thogs med dam tshig ra ba sbyor zhing//dam tshig mchog 'di sgrub pa ni//ye shes rdo rje ngag gis bya//rtse gsum ye shes lcags kyus sogs//
30a3(2): rdo rje khyad par gyis bsgrubs na//sku gsung thugs kyi bsgrub pa yi//bsams gtan des ni 'grub par 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das dam tshig chen po'i dngos grub rdo rjes de skad ces bka' stsal to//
30a4(2): lam gyis bzhi mdo 'am shing gcig drung//mtshan ma gcig dang zhi gnas su//rdo rje dgug pa'i khyad par gyis//sgrub pa pos ni rtag tu bsgrub//sbyor gsum rdo rje can rnams kyi//sbyor gsum sngags kyi 
30a5(2): kyi skyes bu bsam//sangs rgyas ye shes blo ldan gyis//sku gsung thugs kyi kyo ba dang//rlung gi dkyil 'khor mchog gnas pa//sangs rgyas dgug pa dam pa yin//dam tshig las byung phyogs bcu 
30a6(2): pa;rdo rjes bgug gnas nye bar sbyong//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi rdo rje dam tshig dgug pa'i cho ga'o//rnam par snang mdzad 'khor lo che//rdo rje pad+mo la sogs pas//rgyal gnas lcags kyus bsams nas ni//
30a7(2): dam tshig dgug mchog kyo bas bya//khams gsum kyi dam tshig dgug pa'o//rnam pa'i mchog rnams kun ldan pa'i//sangs rgyas sku ni rnams bsgoms la//lag tu sku dang gsung thugs kyi//kyo ba la sog+sa 
30b1(2): rab tu bsgom//'di dag lta bu'i sbyor ba yis//de ni gnas las byed par 'gyur//rnam pa'i mchog rnams kun ldan pa'i//rdo rje sku ni rnams I//bsgoms na//rdo rje'i sbyor bas lce bsgoms na//
30b2(2): rdo rje gsung dang 'dra bar 'gyur//rnam pa'i mchogarnams kun ldan pa'i//sangs rgyas sku ni rnams bsgoms la//lag tu bsgrub bya bsams(na) ni//rdo rje dang ni 'dra bar 'gyur//gsang gsum dam tshig    
30b3(2): mchod pas mchod//mchod pa'i mchod nas rab tu sgrub//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//gsang ba'i snying po bsdus pa yi//bcom ldan 'das gsang ba chen po'i dam tshig gis de skad ces gsungs so//sha chen 
30b4(2): dam tshig mchog gis ni//rdo rje gsum mchog bsgrub par bya//bshang gci dam tshig mchog gis ni/rig pa 'dzin pa'i gtso bor 'gyur//glang po'i sha yi dam tshig gis//mngon shes lnga ngag thob par 'gyur//
30b5(2): rta sha dag dam tshig gis//mi snang ba yi bdag por 'gyur//khyi sha dag gi dam tshig gis nga//dngos grub thams cad 'grub bar 'gyur//ba lang sha yi dam tshig gis//rdo rje dgug pa'i mchogatu//'gyur//
30b6(2): sha rnams thams cad med nas yang//thams cad bsams pas rnam par brtag/rdo rje sbyor ba 'di byas na//sangs rgyas thams cad byin gyis rlob//rnam pa'i mchog rnams kun ldan pa//sku gsung thug+sa
30b7(2): ni rdo rje can//snying khar ye shes dam tshig dang//dbu rgyan rdo rje mchog 'dzin pa//sangs rgyas thams cadamanyes 'gyur pa//dam tshig mchog gis bya ba ni//dngos grub thams cada'agrub pa'i mchog/
32a1(2): @#//dam tshig mchog gis thams bya//dam tshig thams cad ye shes rdo rjer 'gyur//pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//lce yi dam tshig rdo rje mchog//rdo rje can gyis hUM bsgoms te//bdud rtsi lnga yi 
32a2(2): yi sbyor ba yis//zos na rdo rje gsum tho ba 'gyur//AHdang oM gyi dam tshig kyang//'di ni rdo rje'i tshul mchog ste//'di lta bu yi pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzi sbyor ba yis//rdo rje mi ched 'dra bar 'gyur//
32a3(2): rdo rje'i dam tshig bdud rtsi'i 'phreng pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'jin to//!rdo rje dam tshig gsum grub mchog/sku gsum rdo rje can du 'gyur//phyogs bcu'i sems can thams cad kyi//nor yid bzhin rgya mtshor 
32a4(2): 'gyur//'jig rten khams ni thams cad du//rdo rje'i bdag nyid snang bar byed//'khor lo'i dam tshig grub pa'i mchog/sangs rgyas sku dang 'dra bar 'gyur//gang+gA'i bye snyed thams cad du//grub pa kun tu 
32a5(2): rnam par spyod//dam tshig dam pa thams cad kyi//rig pa 'dzin pa'i gtso bor 'gyur//rdo rje'i sku ni ra ba bsgoms pas//thams cad dam tshig mchog tu 'grub//mi snang gyur pa thams cad la//stong   
32a6(2): khams dag ni snang bar byed//sangs rgyas kun las 'phrog pa dang//lha yi bu mo mchog la//gang+gA'i bye snyed sangs rgyas rnams//rdo rje gsum gyi gnas bzhugs pa//rang lga skyu rur bzhag bzhin//
32a7(2): rdo rje mig gis mthong bar 'gyur//gang+gA'i byed stong zhing rnams na//sgra grags pa ni ji snyed pa//mngon shes rna bas thos pa ni//rna ba'i drung na 'khod pa bzhin//gang+gA'i bye snyed zhing dag gi/sems can gyi  
32b1(2): kun gyi sems rnams ni//sku gsung thugs kyi mtshan pa yis//rol mo las byung sems rig 'gyur//bskal pa gang+gA'i bye snyed du/'khor ba dag na gnas/pa yi//sngon gyi gnas kyi gdan tshigs rnams//
32b2(2): zhag gsum lon pa bzhin du dran//gang+gA'i bye ma snyed kyi lus//sangs rgyas sprin la sogs brgyan//bskal pa'i gang+gA'i bye snyed du//rdo rje rdzu 'phrul mchog gis 'gengs//bcom ldan 'das dam tshig mngon
32b3(2): par mkhyen pas 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//de la mngon par shes pa lnga yi ni//rdo rje'i spyan dang rdo rje'i dang snyan dang//rdo rje'i thugs dang//rdo rje'i gnas dang//rdo rje'i gardzu 'phrul lo//sangs rgyas mngon shes
32b4(2): lnga don 'grub//sangs rgyas sku dang 'dra bar 'gyur//gang+gA'i bye snyed grangs med pa'i//'khor gyis yongs su bskor nas su//rdo rje sku dang gsung thugs kyis//'jig rten khams kun bgrod par 'gyur//
32b5(2): bsnyen pa'i dam tshig sbyor ba dang//nye bar sgrub pa 'byung dang//sgrub pa'i don gyi dam tshig dang//sgrub pa chen po dag dang bzhi//rdo rje'i bye brag shes byas la//de nas las rnams bsgrub par bya//bsnyen pa'i
32b6(2): ting nge 'dzin rab sbyor ba//byang chub mchog ni bsgom par bya//nye bar sgrub pa mchog la//rdo rje skye mched rnam par bdapyad//sngags kyi bdag po bsgoms pa yi(sa)//sgrub pa'i tshe na bskul bar gsungs//
32b7(2): sgrub pa chen po byed pa'i tshe//rang sngags rdo rje can gyi gzugs//dbu rgyan la ni bdag po bsam//ye shes rdo rje 'grus par 'gyur//bsnyen pa'i ye shes bdud rtsi dag/thams cad du ni rtag par bya//'di ni
34a1(2): @#//rnam par mi rtog yang dag rig//ye shes 'byung ba rab tu bshad//yid 'ong sngags rnams kun bsdus pa//rdo rje bzlas pa ye shes che//sku gsum mi phyed dkyil 'khor dang//
34a2(2): sangs rgyas ye shes thob 'gyur ba//rdo rje bzlas pa ye shesache//sku gsum mi byed dkyil 'khor dang//sangs rgyas ye shes thob 'gyu ba//rdo rje mi phyed gsum bsgoms pa'i//rdo rje bzlas 
34a3(2): pa'i sbyor ba yis//sangs rgyas kyi kun gyis byin gyis rlob//rigs dang gsang ba sngags thams cad kyi//sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi mtshan//gsang sngags zlos pa de ring gsungs//ye shes rgya mtsho 
34a4(2): gsan pa dang//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje can//sangs rgyas dus gsum las byung rnams//rdo rje sngags ni rab bsgoms pas//ye shes mnyam med thob 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das kyis bshad 
34a5(2): du gsol//de nas ston pa rdo rje 'dzin//nam mkha' rdo rje ye shes 'byung//rdo byed skyed dam pa mchog gi gtsos//rdo rje bzlas pa 'di gsungs so//sngags kyi don kun bzlas pa ni//rdo rje mi phyed gsum
34a6(2): gyis mtshan//rdo rje bye ba gsum gyi mthar//bkod pa rdo rje gsum zhes bya//sku gsung thugs kyi gnas rnams su//spro ba rnam pa gsum dag bya//rdo rje bzlas pa 'di yis ni//rdo rje gsum gyi 
34a7(2): thugs 'drar 'gyur//sangs rgyas rnams kyi sku gsung thugs//bsgoms na mchod pa'i mchod brtag pa//ye shes rdo rje byas na ni//rdo rje gsum gyi thugs 'drar 'gyur//sangasargyas rnam+sa 
34b1(2): kyi sku gsung thugs//bsams na mchod pa'i mchog brtag pa//ye shes rdo rje rab tu bya//'di ni byang chub mchog thob pa'o//yang na dbye ba rnam gsum gyis//so so dag tu spro bar bya//sku dang gsung 
34b2(2): thugs bdag med pa//ye shes sems kyis yang dag spro//brjod na rdo rje spro ba ni//rdzogs na rab tu bsdu bar bya//'di ni ye shes spyan phye ba'i//sangs rgyas rnams ni kun gyi yin//sku yi rdo rje rdzog+sa 
34b3(2): byang chub//dngos dang dngos med rnam dpyad pa//sangs rgyas sku zhes bya bar bshad//de ni sku yi bzlas par bshad//gsung gi dam tshig rdzogs byang chub//sgra dang sgra min rnam dpyad pa//
34b4(2): gsung gi rdo rje zhes bshad do//de ni gsung gi bzlas par bshad//thugs kyi dam tshig rdzogs byang chu ba//gnas pa'i rdo rje rnam dpyad pa//thugs kyi bzlas par bshad//de ni thugs kyi bzlas 
34b5(2): par bshad//don gyi rjes 'gro bzlas pa na//ngo bo nyid med bzang po yis//dus gsum sangs rgyas rnams dpyad pa//de ni rin chen bzlas par bshad//sangs rgyas zhing rnams thams cad du//
34b6(2): sku yi sprin rnams spro ba dang//bzhung cing byon pa rdo rje don//de ni thogs med bzlas par bshad//khro bo'i dam tshig ye shes kyis//rang gi rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor grags//sngags kyi yi 
34b7(2): ge'i tshig thos pa//de ni khro bo'i bzlas par bshad//'dod pa'i don gyis myos gyur pa//kun tu skyob cing phan par dga'//sems can gti mug gnas la 'jog/de ni gti 
35a1(2): @#//mug bzlas par bshad//'dod chags rdo rje las byung tshig/lus dang ngag sems la gnas pa'i//sems can 'dod chags mtshor gnas gzhag/de ni 
35a2(2): 'dod chags bzlas par bshad//zhe sdang rdo rje las byung sems//lus dang ngag sems la gnad pa'i//sems can zheng sdang gnas la gzhag/de ni zhe sdang bzlas par bshad//
35a3(2): dam tshig de nyid rdo rje gsum//dbu ma dam tshig rdo rje can//de nyid dam tshig rdo rje yi//bzlas pa mi nang zhes bya'o//'dod chags de nyid don bsgoms pa//rdo rje bdag po thams cad ni//
35a4(2): sems can kun la phan 'dod pa'i//'dod chags byang chub rdzogs par mdzad//rig pa chen po spyan la sogs//rtag tu 'dod pa'i don brtson pa//'dod pa'i longs spyod dag gis ni//ji ltar 
35a5(2): 'dod pa bsten na 'grub//gti mug dam tshig las byung ba//rig pa'i rgyal po rdo rje can//mi nang ngag gi gnas grub pa//dngos grub dam pa rab tu ste ra//khro bo zhe sdang gnas las skyes//
35a6(2): bsod la rtag tu brtson pa dag/sgrub pa mchog gi chos can la//gsod pa'i don gyis 'grub par 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das skyes bu chen po'i dam tshig gis de skad ces bka' stsal to//
35a7(2): ye shes 'khor lo can rnams kyi//'khor lo snying kha'i dbus su bsgom//gsal ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//dbus su 'khor lo'i don bsgom mo//ye shes rdo rje can rnams kyi//
35b1(2): rdo rje snying kha'i dbus su bsgom//rdo rje dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//rdo rje'i sngags don bsgom par bya//rin chen ye shes can rnams kyi//rin chen snying kha'i dbus su bsgom par bya//
35b2(2): rin chen dkyil 'khor dbus gnas bar//rin chen sngags don bsgom par bya //pad+mo ye shes can rnams kyi/pad+mo snying kha'i dbus su bsgom/ /chos kyi dkyil 'khor dbus gnas (   )par//
35b3(2): pad+mo'i sngags don bsgom par (   )bya//ral gri ye shes can rnams kyi/ /ral gri snying kha'i dbus su bsgom//dam tshig dkyil 'khor dbus gnas(   )par//ral gri sngags don bsgom par bya(   )bya//
35b4(2): dkyil 'khor kun gyi ngos rnams su//sangs rgyas lnga rnams dgod par bya//'od gzer lnga yi bye brag gis//spros na byang chub thob par 'gyur//sngags rnams thams cad spro ba ni//bye brag 
35b5(2): rnam pa Sgnyis su bsgrags//sku gsung rdo rje bye brag gis//spro ba dang ni bsdu ba bya//lus kyis sku yi ngo bo nyid//sems kyis thugs kyi ngo bo nyid//ngag gis gsung gi ngo bo nyid//
35b6(2): mchod nas mchod pa'i gnas su 'gyur//dkyil 'khor chen po'i 'khor lo yis//rdo rje lnga rnams bsgom par bya//dbus su bdag po bsams nas ni//sku gsung rdo rje rang gi gzugs//
35b7(2): rang sngags rdo rje'i gzugs dag gis//dkyil 'khor rnam pa bzhi dag tu//kha dog rnam bzhir kun brtags la//snying khar sngags kyi don bsgoms na//rdo rje rnam pa bzhi yi las//bsam
36a1(2): @#//gtan rdo rje can dag byed//'di na sngags rnams thams cad kyi//gsang ba rab tu bzang po yin//zhi ba la ni spyan gyi tshul//rgyas la rdo rje pad+mo can//dbang 
36a2(2): la rnam par (snang)mdzad gnas//rdo rje khro bo mngon spyod la//'di ni rdo rje thams cad kyi//gsang pa sku gsum las byung ba//sngags rnams kun ni sprul pa ste//bya ba'i rol mos mtshan pa'o//ma dad smra ba'i 
36a3(2): sems can dang/rdo rje bslob dpon smod pa dang//gdug pa'i sems can gzhan rnams la'ang//rab tu bskul ba 'di bya'o//bcom ldan 'das ye shes chen po'i 'khor lo rdo rjes de skad ces 
36a4(2): bka' stsal to//khams gsum gnas pa'i sems can rnams//sangs rgyas skur ni rnam bsgoms la//dgra bos bsad par bsams nas su//de nas las rnams brtsam par bya//
36a5(2): nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//rdo rje rtse lnga kha bzhi pa//rnam pa'i mchog rnams kun dang ldan//rdo rje sems dpar rnam par bsgom//dus gsum dam tshig las byung ba//sangs rgyas 
36a6(2): 'khor lo rnams bsgoms la//sangs rgyas 'khor lo stobs po che//lag pa g.yas pas 'di bya'o//phyogs bcu nas byung sems can rnams//sangasargyas sku yi bye brag gis//bsdus 
36a7(2): te gong bu'i tshul gyis su//rang gi lus la de dag bzung//sangs rgyas ye shes 'khor lo can//slar yang spro ba rab tu bya//khro bas 'khrugs pa'i khro bo rnams//mi sdug 'jigs su 
36b1(2): rung ba'i gzugs//mtshon cha sna tshogs mchog 'dzin pa//gsod ba'i don gyis don sems pa//rdo rje sems dpa' rang nyid dam//gdugs pa chen po gsod bar byed//sangs rgyas 
36b2(2): sku gsum mchog stsol ba'o//rdo rje gsum gyi mchog dkyil 'khor gnas//nyi ma bdun du 'di byas na//sangs rgyas dngos grub ster bar 'gyur//rmongs pas grub pa mi ster na//'chi bar 'di 
36b3(2): ni gdon mi za//rdo rje'i dam tshig ye shes kyi bka'i 'khor lo zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus su ni//'khor lo rdo rje 'bar bas brgyan//rnam pa'i mchog ni kun 
36b4(2): dang ldan//nam par snang mdzad rab tu bsgom//dus gsum dam tshig las byung ba//rdo rje sems dpa' grags chen ni//rdo rje me stag 'phro ba can//de yi phyag na bsnams par bsgom//
36b5(2): phyogs bcu nas byung sems can rnams//rdo rje sku yi khyad bar gyis//'od gzer tshul gyis bsdus nas ni//de dag bdag gi lus la bzung//spro ba rdo rje thams cad kyi//ye shes mchog 
36b6(2): gi gnyen gyis bya//sku gsung thugs kyi sbyor ba can//sangs rgyas thams cad bdag la gson//bdag ni dpal ldan rdo rje 'dzin//bka' yi 'khor lo rab sbyor ba//gal te dam tshig 
36b7(2): las 'das na//rdo rje rab tu 'bar ba yis//sku gsum las byung dges bar bgyi//zhig par 'gyur bar gdon mi 'tshal//'khor lo'i dam tshig ye shes rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//
37a1(2): @#//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor rdo rje can//namakha' rdo rje'i dbus su bsam//gshin rje gshed kyi 'khor lo che//rang gi rdo rje yin par brtag/dus gsum     
37a2(2): sangs rgyas sems dpa' rnams//sku gsum dkyil 'khor bzhugs pa dag/gshin rje gshed kyi mdog 'dra ba//slar yang sangs rgyas spro bar bya//dus gsum la zhugs sem+sa  
37a3(2): can dang//gdug sems can gyi dgra bo rnams//khros pas thams cad gsod par bsgom//'di ni rdo rje bka'i dkyil 'khor dam tshig thams cad las byung ba gshin rje gshed kyi sku    
37a4(2): gsum gyi ye shes rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin no//sku gsum thugs kyi rdo rje 'am//rang sngags don gyi yon tan nam//yang na gtsug tor dam tshig gis//bka' yi 'khor lo sbyar 
37a5(2): bar bya//sngags kun srung phyir ye shes mchog/rdo rje can gyis bya ba ste//'di ni rdo rje thams cad kyi//byang chub bsrung ba'i don chen po//bcom ldan 'das sku rdo rjes de skad 
37a6(2): ces bka' stsal to//nam mkha' rdo rje'i dbus gnas par//chos kyi snang mdzad sku mchog las//byung ba'i sku gsum dam tshig/dag/mkha' dbyings rdo rje'i dbus gan+sa 
37a7(2): par//bsams nas gdan du der ba brtag/mkha' dbyings sangs rgyas thams cad kyis//yongs su gang bar rnams par bsgom//rang sngags yi ge'i gnas ye shes//
37b1(2): thugs kyi rnam par bsgom par bya//sangs rgyas rnams ni sems tsam du//bsgoms nas slar yang rab tu bsdu//thugs kyi rdo rjer byas pa'i phyir//sku gsum la ni de dag gzung+sa//
37b2(2): rdo rje'i sngags rin po che rab tu snang bar byed pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//rnam pa'i mchog rnams kun ldan ba'i//rdo rje sems dpa' rab tu bsgom//sangs rgyas rim bzhin bgod nas ni//
37b3(2): chu yi steng du bcag par bya//rkang pa mnyam gzhag sbyor ba yis//spyi bo'i steng du rkang pa bsgom//bcom ldan 'das ngo bo nyid dag pas 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rdo rje rgya mtsho rkang pas  
37b4(2): gnon pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzani to//dbang chen dkyil 'khor bsgoms nas ni//dbus su khro bo rnams gzhag la//las kyi rdo rje rkang pa yis//de yi spyi bor gnon par bsgom//mu stegs 
37b5(2): can dang pas kyis rgol ba thams cad kyi 'khrul 'khor gnon pa zhes bya ba'i ting de 'dzin to//rdo rje gsum mchog khro bo'i tshul//ge sar ser po'i mdog 'dra ba//thams cad ri yi rgyal po ltar//
37b6(2): bsams nas spyi bor rab tu bsgom//sangs rgya dbung gnon byed na//mi dbang la ni dogs pa med//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs las 
37b7(2): byung bas 'di skad ces bka' stsalato//dpung thams cad gnon pa zhes bya ba'i tingade 'dzin to//dgra bo sgrag pa'i dam tshig gis//bsam gtan rab tu brtag pa 'di//sangs rgya(sa)
38a1(2): ka/@#//ngag gis 'das na yang //'gas par 'gyur bar gdon mi za//bcom ldan 'das rdo rje gsum gyi dam tshig gis de skad ces gsungs so//hUM las phur bu  
38a2(2): bsam pa ni//rtse lnga pa yi tshad du ste//rdo rje phur bu de yis ni//snying khar de ni rab bsgoms na//khros pas sangs rgyas dpung dag kyang //'jigs par 'gyur ba gadon mi za//
38a3(2): dgra rmongs par byed pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//grong ngam yang na grong khyer ram//ljongs dag tu ni rab sbyor ba//nad kun spyod pa'i zhi ba yang //'dis ni rtag tu sbyar bar bya//rdo rje rtse mo lnga 
38a4(2): pa ni//bar snang dag lar btu bsgom//sregs pa'i bskal pa ltar bsams na//slar yang rab tu bsdu bar bya//spro ba dag kyang de nas bya//rin chen yid bzhin nor bu'i 'od//
38a5(2): chos kyi sprin ni bsgoms nas kyang //dbang bskur ba ni yang dag bya//rdo rje'i bsam gtan 'di yis ni//da gang bka' ba yang rab tu 'gangs//sbyin pa'i rdo rje rab tu bsgom//yid bzhin
38a6(2): nor bu dpal ldan 'gyur//sangs rgyas sprin dang chos chen dang //rdo rje sems dpa' der spro bya//bskal pa gsum gyi yun gnas shing //sangs rgyas thams cad byin gyis brlob//
38a7(2): 'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//gsang ba'i sku ste skyon med pa'o//nad thams cad med par byed pa rdo rje 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//bsam gtan las byung dam bcas pas//
38b1(2): gnas gang du ni yang dag spyad//bsam gtan sbyor ba 'di yis ni//gnas na sangs rgyas byin gyis brlob//rdo rje bdud rtsi rgyal po che//rdo rje phur bu rab tu bsgom//me stag 'bar ba 'dra 
38b2(2): ba yis//phyogs bcu'i dkyil 'khor gang ba par bya//'gro ba 'dul bzhi ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//zhi ba'i dkyil 'khor dam pa ni//nam mkha'i dbyings kyi dbus su bsam//
38b3(2): rnam par snang mdzad gzugs bsam nas//rang gi thugs khar 'dod pa bzhag//spyan la sogs pa mchog rnams kyis//namakha' dbyings yongs su gang bar bsgom//bsdus na 'od kyi gong 
38b4(2): bu yis//nad pa la ni dbab par bya//ba spu'i bu gnas sangs rgyas//sprin rnams brtul zhugs can gyis spro//sangs rgyas sprin rnams de yi tshe//de la dbang bskur rab tu stsol//nad 
38b5(2): pa rdo rje dam tshig gis//skad cig gis ni dpal ldan 'gyur//sangs rgyas kyi dam tshig bkod pa'i sprin zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzinado//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus su ni//dbang chen dkyil 
38b6(2): 'khor dam pa bsam//chos 'dzin pa yi gzugs bsams nas//snying khar 'dod pa bzhag par bya//mkha' dbyings gnas ni nyams dga' bar//dkar mo zhes byas rab tu dgang//
38b7(2): 'od kyi gong bur bsdus nas ni//yid bzhin nor bu rin chen 'od//lus dang ngag dang sems gnas la//'dod pa la ded bab par bya//ba spu'i bug nas rin chen//sprin rnams brtul 
40a1(2): @#//mdangs//ser dang dkar dang gsal ba'i zhal//bsgoms ni ye shes thob par 'gyur//khro bo chen gshin rje gshed//zhal gsum drag po'i 'od bzang ba//rnon po'i 
40a2(2): po 'jigs pa'i 'jigs par byed//kha dog nag po rab tu bsgom//gzhan gyis mi thub khro bo che//drag tu bzhad pa'i sgra 'byin pa//zhal gsum 'od gzer sna tshogs dag//rab tu 'phro ba rnam par   
40a3(2): bsam//khro bo chen po rnam grin ni//bskal pa'i me bzhin rab tu 'bar//zhal gsum gdug pa zhabs kyi mnan//sbyor ba can gyis rtag tu bsgom//rdo rje mi mchi kho bo che//'od gzer 'phro 
40a4(2): ba rab tu 'bar//drag po 'khor lo 'bar ba 'dra//'jigs pa dag kyang 'jigs par byed//'dod pa'i ye shes khro bo che//zhal gsum pa ni 'jigs par byed//phyag bzhi 'jigs par byed 
40a5(2): pa'i mchog//Tak+ki rgyal po rab tu bsgom//stobs po chen ni rdo rje che//'jig rten gsum gyi mun laM sel ba//gdug pa thams cad 'joms par byed//zhal gsum par ni rtag par bsgom//
40a6(2): khro bo chen po dbyig ston can//khams gsum dag ni 'jigs par byed//zhal gsum rdo rje gsum las byung//'bar ba rnon po can du bsgom//rdo rje mi g.yo khro bo che//rdo rje las byung mi g.yo 
40a7(2): zhi ba ral gri zhags pa bsnams//brtul zhugs can gyis zhal gsum bsgom//gtsu gtor chen po yi ge gcig //'od gzer dag ni kun tu 'phro//zhal gsum 'bar ba 'khor lo can//
40b1(2): bsam gtan dkyil 'khor bsgom par byed//mnod mdzes ye shes mchog 'dzin pa//'jigs pa'i rgya mtsho yang dag byed//zhal gsum 'bar ba 'od 'phro can//bsam gtan dkyil 'khor bsgaum par bya//
40b2(2): gzi brjid phung po gtsugator rgyal//gang gzhan sngags kyi 'khor lo can//dam tshig longs spyod 'di rnams kyis//so so dag tu bsgom par bya//ting nge 'dzin ni dpag med pa//sngags kyi ni bshad pa yin//
40b3(2): khro bo dag ni re re yang//namakha'i steng du shin tu 'phags//namakha'i dbyings kyi dbus gnas par//gsal ba'i dkyil 'khor dam pa bsam//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rab bsgoms la//rnam par 
40b4(2): snang mdzad rnam par bsam//gsal zhing zhi ba zla ba bzhin//'od gzer sna tshogs 'dra ba'i 'od//me long lta bu yang dag byung//khams gsum dag ni dkyil 'khor du//rgyan rnams kun gyis brgyan 
40b5(2): brgyan bdag/bsams ni de yis byang chub mthong//bsam gtan rdo rje ra ba bsgom pa//'di yis sangs rgyas che ba'i gabadag/'jig rten thams cad dbang byed pa//tshe 'di nyid kyis 'tho ba

41a1(2): ka @#//kun gyi rnam par brgyan//zhi ba'i ye shes gnas bsgoms na//myur du rdo rje nyid thob 'gyur//bsam gtan rdo rje rab bskul ba//'dis ni rdo rje'i nyid bdag 
41a2(2): nyid che//sems can thams cad dbang byed pa//tshe 'di nyid la thob par 'gyur//dam tshig thams cad 'byung ba mdzes pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//chos kyi dkyil 'khor dam pa//
41a3(2): nam mkha'i rdo rje'i dbus su bsam//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rab bsgom la//chos kyi sems dpa' rnam par bsgom//gsal ba'i lus 'dzin zhi ba ni//rgyan rnams kun gyis brnam 
41a4(2): par brgyan//'od gzer sprin gyi 'khor lo che//rnam par 'phro ba rab tu bsgom//'di yis chos kyi bdag nyid che//sku gsum mi phyed las byung ba//ye shes rgya mtshos rnam par

41a6(2): bsam//sangs rgyas gzugasani rab bsgoms la//spyan gyi mchog ni rnam par bsgom//zhal bzang spyan ni rab tu yangs//sna tshogs rgyan gyis rnam par brgyan//mtshan rnams 
41a7(2): thams cad rab tu rdzogs//bud med sgyu ma'i mchog ldan pa//khams gsum dag ni dbang byed pa//ye shes thams cad grub par byed//'khor lo yid bzhin nor bu ster//
41b1(2): phyag tu 'khor lo bsgom par bya//spyan gyi dam pa tshig phyag gi mchog dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor 'od mchog can//mkha' dbyings rdo rje'i dbus su 
41b2(2): bsam//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rab bsgoms nas//nam mkha'i rdo rje rab mchog bsgom//zhal bzang spyan ni rab tu yangs//ut+pal mthing ka'i mdog 'dra ba//mtshan rnams 
41b3(2): thams cad rab tu rdzogs//nam mkha' yum gyi rin po che//'od kyi sprin rdo rje tshim par byed pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'jin to//sangs rgyas byang chub byed pa'i mchog/dngos grub rdo rje 
41b4(2): can gyi gsang //ut+pal sdo dmar phyag na bsnams//nam mkha' yum gyi ri po che//'od kyi sprin rdorje tshim par byed pa zhes byas ba'i ting nge 'dzin to/chos kyi dkyil 'khor dam pa ni//
41b5(2): nam mkha'i rdo rje'i dbus su bsam//sangs rgyas gzug ni rab bsgoms la//rdo rje chos ma rab tu bsgom//zhal bzang spyan na rab tu yangs//pad+mar ga'i mdog 'dra ba'i//
41b6(2): sgyu ma mtshan pa'i mchog las byung //'dod chags chags pa 'dzin sdug pa//mtshan nyid thams cad yongs rdzogs shing //rgyan rnams kun gyis brgyan pa yis//sangs rgyas thams 
41b7(2): cad rab bsgoms pa//chos kyi ye shes 'byung gnas mchog//dam tshig rdo rje can gyi gsang //ut+pa la dmar po phyag na bsnams//chos kyi dam tshig de kho nyid mngon par byang chub 
42a1(2): @#//pa kun tu ston pa ting nge 'dzin to//dngos grub dkyil 'khor dam pa ni//nam mkha'i rdo rje'i dbus su bsam//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rab bsgoms la//
42a2(2): sgrol ma'i mchog ni bsgom par bya//zhal bzas spyan ni rab tu yangs//sna tshogs rgyan gyis rnam par brgyan//kha dog ljang ser 'dra ba'i 'od//bud med sgyu ma rab tu myos//rdo rje ting  
42a3(2): nge 'dzin las byung//sems can kun gyis phyag byas pa//ut+pa la ser po'i mdog 'dra ba'i//gsal ba phyag na bsnams par bsgom//dam tshig sgrol ma mchog dang ldan pa zhes bya 
42a4(2): bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dam pa ni/nam mkha'i rdo rje'i dbus subasam//sangs rgyas gzugasani rab bsgoms la//gshin rje gshed mchog rnam par bsgom//
42a5(2): 'bar ba'i 'od gzer mang po 'phro//'jigs pa'i dkyil 'khor rab tu khros//spyan mar mche ba rnam par gtsigs//phyag na ral gri bsnams par bsgom//snang mdzad dbu rgyan rnam   
42a6(2): bsgoms na//rdo rje can ni dgyes par 'gyur//'di ni ye shes rdo rje can//khro bo kun gyi dam tshig yin//gshin rje gshed kyi spro ba'i snang ba bkod pa bsgom pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 
42a7(2): 'dzin to//nyi ma'i dkyil mkhor dam pa ni//nam mkha'i rdo rje'i dbus du bsam//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rab bsgoms la//gzhan gyis mi thub par bsgoms pa//'bar ba'i 
42b1(2): 'od gzer mang po can//sprul gyis brgyan zhing ska rags can//rdo rje dgyes pa 'jigs 'jigs lta//kha dog dkar por btu bsgom//mi bskyod dam tshig dbu rgyan la//bsams na rdo rje 
42b2(2): can dag mnyes//'di ni ye shes blo can gyi//khro bo kun gyi dam tshig go/gzhan gyis mi thub pa'i rdo rje bkod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dam pa ni//nam mkha'i 
42b3(2): rdo rje'i dbus su bsam//sangs rgyas gzugs ni rab bsgoms la//rdo rje rta ni rab tu bsgom//khro bo'i 'od gzer mang po dag/thams cad du ni rnam par 'phro//gdug pa thams cad zhabs 
42b4(2): kyis mnan//kha dog dmar po rnam par bsam//rdzogs sangs dpag tu med dbu rgyan la//bsgoms na rdo rje can//khro bo kun gyi dam tshigs yin//rta bskyed pa 'byung ba rnam par bkod 
42b5(2): pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dz-in to//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khod dam pa ni//nam mkha'i rdo rje'i dbus su bsam/sangs rgyas gzugs ni ra ba bsgom la//rdo rje ma'i chi rab tu bsgom//'bar ba'i 'od 
42b6(2): gzer mang po 'phro//rdo rje'i sprin rnams kun tu 'khrug/khros shing khro bcas gnag pa dang//mche ba rnon por btu bsgom//mi bskyod dam tshig dbu rgyan la//bsgoms nas khro bo 'dzin 
42b7(2): mnyes 'gyur//'di ni khro bo thams cad kyi//'da' bar dka' ba'i dam tshig go࿒ /mi 'chi ba'i dam tshig 'byung ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khaur dam pa ni//
45a1(2): @#//rjes su dran//rtag tu chos rgyas stobs mdzad pa//rtag tu mdzod po chen dang ldan//rdo rje gsung gi bka' bzhin du//bzlas pa tsam gyis byed par 'gyur//
45a2(2): de nas bcom ldan 'das dam tshig kun nas 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs nas//dam tshig sems dpa'i btsun mo'i mchog 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las 
45a3(2): byung ngo //oM tA re tut+tA re tu re swA hA//de nas 'di ni gsungs tsam gyis//sangs rgyas kun bdag che las skyes//mnyes shing ye shes thob gyur na//rdo rje ni sku ni rjes su dran//sangs rgyas rdo rje 
45a4(2): sde chen dang //sems can khams ni ma lus dpa//skad cig gis ni bran bzhin/du//thams cad g.yo med dbang du byed//ces bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das 
45a5(2): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thubs rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa//dri ma med pa'i 'od gzer gyi sprin zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzinal snyoms par zhugs nas//rdo rje gshin rje'i bshed po 
45a6(2): chen po rdo rje khro bo 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsang dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo //na ma:sa man+ta ka ya wA ka tsit+ta badzra NAM//oM kha kha khA hi khA hi/sarba du Sh+TA sa twa da ma ka//a si mu sa la/par shu/pA sha hasta/
45a7(2): tsa tur b+hu dza/tsa tur mu kha/Tha Tu ra Ni/A gats+tsha A gats+tsha/sarba du Sh+TA prA Na A pa hA ri Ne//ma hA big+h+nan g+ha ta ka/bi kri nA/sarba b+hu ta//b+ha ya ga ra/aT+Ta Sh+Ta ha sa nA di ne/byA g+p+ra tsa rma ni ba sa na/kun 
45b1(2): ru kus ru sarba karmA Ti//tshin+d+ha tshin+d+ha sarba man trAn//bin+d+ha bin+d+ha pa ra mung drAm/A kaR+ShA ya A kaR+Sha sarba b+hU ta naM//ma tha ma tha/nirma tha nirmaM/sarba duSh+Tan/pra ba sha ya pra be sha ya maNA lam d+h+ye//be ba swA ta/dzI bi 
45b2(2): tA na to ka ram ma sarba ka r+yaM ku ru ku ru//da ha da ha/pa tsa pa tsa/mA bi lam ba mA bi laM ba//sa ma ya ma nu smrar/hUM hUM phaT phaT//bi s+pho Ta yi bi s+pho Ta ya/sarba na nA sha ya//ka ra ka ra bi g+h+naM/sarba a shwa 
45b3(2): pa ri pU ra ka/he he b+ha ga wan//kany+tsi rA ya si/ma ma sarba a thA na/sA d+ha na swA hA//de nas 'di ni gsungs tsam gyis//sangs rgyas grags pa chen po kun/'jigs shing kun tu skrag gyur nas//rdo rje sem+sa 
45b4(2): dpa' rjes su dran/thod pa mchag mags pa/gzugs mdzes pa yid 'od rnyed pa la//rkang pas mnan pa'i tshul byas nas//sngags 'di rjes su dran par bya//mAm kI'am spyan yang rung //rdo rje'i rigs chen l+sa 
45b5(2): byung ba//lan gsum dag tu brjod na ni//de ma thag tu nges par 'gugs/bcom ldan 'das thugs rdo rjes su de skad ces gsungs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par (snang)mdzad/
45b6(2): dam tshig gi la 'od gzer stug po'i mchog ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//khro bo mi 'chi ba'i dam tshig 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//na maHsa man+ti kA wA ka ci 
45b7(2): t+ta badzra tAM//na mo badzra kro d+hA ya/ma hA dam Sh+Ta Ta ka Ta b+her b+ha ya/a si mu sa la pa ru shu pA sha ha stA sa ya/oM a mri tri kun+Ta li//kha kha khA hi khA hi/tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha/ban+d+ha ban+d+ha/ha na ha na/da ha da ha/gardza gardza/bi s+pho Ta 
45'a1(2): @#//ya bi s+phoT ya//sarba big+ha bi nA ya kA na ma hA ga Na ba ti dzI bi ta na rA ya swA hA//de nas 'di ni gsungs tsam gyis//sangs rgyas grags pa chen po kun//brgyal zhing rab 
45'a2(2): tu 'jigs gyur na//rdo rje'i sku ni sa rjes su dran//sngags rnams kun gyi sbyor ba dang//rdo rje skad pa'i las rnams la//sangs rgyas nyid kyi dpung dag kyang//tshul bzhin byas na skrod par 'gyur//de nas 
45'a3(2): bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rin chen po tog/sangs rgyas kyi 'od gzer rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/rdo rje kho bo gzhan gyis mi thub pa 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung  
45'a4(2): dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//nam:sa man+ta ka ya wa ka tsit+ta badzrA tAM/oM hUM dzi na ri Ti TA hUM hUM hUM phaT phaT swA hA//de nas 'dini gsungs tsam gyis//sang rgyas bdag nyid che skyes kun//'jigs shing kun tu 
45'a5(2): skrag gyur te//byang chub sems ni gsungs tsam gyis//sangs rgyas bdag nyid cho skyes kun//'jigs shing kun tu skrag gyur te//byang chub sems ni rjes su dran//srin po rungs pa gtum po dag/
45'a6(2): 'jigs pa chen po 'khrugs pa dang//rdo rje sems kyis rab sbyar na//las rnams cho ga bzhin du byed//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhinagashegs pa tshe dpag tu med/pa//dpag tu med pa rdo rje 'byung ba 
45'a7(2): zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs nas//pad+mo 'byung ba rdo rje khro bo chen po 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//ram:saman+ta ka ya wA kA tsit+ta badzra NAM/oM hUM hUM hUM/ta ru la/
45'b1(2): bi ru la/sarba bi Sha g+hA ta ka/dz+wa la ti bi spu li ga/aT+Ta T+Ta hA swA/ke sa ris Ta a To pa badzra khur nir g+ha dan tsa li ti ba su d+ha ta la/ri shwA sa mA ru to ta k+Shi ba ta d+ha ra NI d+har//bi Sha Na a T+Ta T+Ta hA sa//a par mi ta 
45'b2(2): ba la pa rA kra ma//a d+ya ga Na b+hi ta b+hi ga NA/d+hu Shi ta/b+hu d+ya b+hu d+h+ya//ha ya grI ba/khA da khA nga/par man+tan/chin+d+ha chin+d+ha/si d+hi ma me di sha/A be sha ya//sarba dz+wa ra bi shA tsAn/sarba gra he/Shu a pra ti ha to b+ha ba//
45'b3(2): badzra da Sh+TaM kany+tsa rA ya si/i dam du Sh+TA sarba ba//d+hur d+hu na/ma tha ma tha/mar dmar da/pA Ta ya pA Ta ya//ma Ta ma Ta/ban+d+ha ban+d+ha/bu d+ha d+harma badzra saM g+ha a nu dz+nyA taM ka mA ka ru//ha ya gri ba ya phaT//badzra ya 
45'b4(2): phaT/badzra nAM gA trA ya phaT/badzra ne trA ya phaT//badzra daM khu rA ya phaT/badzra khur ni ra g+hA ta na ya phaT/trai lo kya b+ha ba yaM ka rA ya phaT/sarba garmu Sha a bra ti he tA ya/badzra ku la san trAs na ya phaT/hUM hUM hUM 
45'b5(2): phaT phaT phaT/swA hA//de nas 'di ni gsungs tsam gyis//chos kyi rdo rje che mchog skyes//'jigs shing rab tu brgyal bar 'gyur//ye shes rgyal po rjes su dran//mkha'i dbyings dug gi yongs 
45'b6(2): gang ba/rdo rje ha la ha la'i 'od//khro bo'i rgyal po bskul ba yis//dug rnams thams cad med par byed//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa gdon mi za bar grub pa gdon mi ze ba'i dam 
45'b7(2): tshig 'byung ba dpal rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//rdo rje be con sngon po khro bo'i rgyal po 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//namaHsaman+ta kA ya wA ki ci 
47a1(2): @#//po'i dam tshig 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//nam:sa man+ta kA ya wA kA tsit+ta badzrA NA/a tsa la/kA NA/tsaN+Da nat+ta/mo T+Ta mo T+Ta/shaT+Ta shaT+Ta/taT+Ta taT+Ta/
47a2(2): han han/dan had ha/mo ha so ha/mo ha ka ra/ha sa ha sa/badzra ha saM ku ru/ha sa ha sa/mar ta ra ta mar da ra Ta/gardza gardza/ha na ha na/ban+d+ha ban+d+ha/tiSh+Tha tiSh+Tha A be sha A be sha/ma hA man+ta pa la ka/d+hu na d+hu na/
47a3(2): ti Ni ti Ni/khA da khA da/big+ha naM mA ra ya ma ra ya/duSh+TaM/bak+Sha bak+Sha/sarbA ku ru ku ru/ki ri ki ri/ma hA bi Tha Na/badzra s+pho Ta ya s+pho Ta ya/hU~M hU~M hU~M/tri pa li/ra ga na ra ta ka/A+o~M A~M A~M/hU~M hU~M hUM/a tsa la tse Ta/
47a4(2): s+pho Ta ya s+pho Ta ya/hrUM hUM/a sa man+ti ka/tra Ta ma hA bA la kAta ya/pa ra man trAM/oM A+oM/hU~M hU~M ma~M/b+h+ya du lo ka/tu sh+ya tu badzrI/na mo s+t+wo a pra ti ha ta ba le b+h+ya/dza la ya/tra Ta a sa ha na na ma swA hA//de nas 
47a5(2): 'di ni gsungs tsam gyis//lha rnams 'khor du bcas pa kun//brgyal zhing yid ni ra ba skrag nas//rdo rje sku ni rjes su dran//khro bo chen po'i sngags 'di yis//lha chen dge la sogs pa'i lha//
47a6(2): mthu chen dag kyang skrag gyur nas/kha sbyar lus su 'gugs par 'gyur//de nas bcom ldan 'das dam tshig rnam par 'khrul pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin pa snyoms par zhugs nA//rdo rje 'dzin thams 
47a7(2): cad kyi dam tshig khro bo chen po mchod mdzes 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las byung ngo//oM sum+b+ha ni sum+b+ha hU~M//gri h+Na gri h+Na hUM/gri h+Na pa ya gri sta pa ya hUM/A na ya to b+ha ga waM ba d+hI rA dza hU~M phaT/de nas 'di ni 
47b1(2): gsungs tsam gyis//bu mo mthu chen thams cad ni//skra bshig gos dang bral gyur nas//rdo rje sems dpa' rjes su dran//rdo rje sems dpas zhabs kyi mnan//de bzhin gshegs pa kun bdag 
47b2(2): po'i//rdo rje lkugs kyu zhags pa yis//bu mo rnams ni 'gugs pa'i mchog/de nas bcom ldan 'das dam tshig chen po'i de kho na nyid byung ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//
47b3(2): dam tshig chen po'i rdo rje gsum gyi gsung ba gsung gi dam tshig gi de kho na nyid kyi gnas 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//sangs rgyas rdo rje sku gsum la//rdo rje sems dpa' rnam par  
47b4(2): bsgom//zhags pa rdo rje kyo ba 'dzin//sangs rgyas dgug pa dam pa'o//sangs rgyas sku gsung sbyor ba yi//'khor lo chen po sbyor ba yi//rdo rje sems dpa' rgyal po che//rtag tu nges par khrug par 'gyur//
47b5(2): mi phyed rdo rje gsum bsgoms pas//'khor lo pad+mo rdo rje che//rdo rje kyo ba'i khyad par gyis//sngags rnams thams cad dgug pa'i mchog/thams cad rdo rje'i dngos zhi ba//'khor lo pad+mo rdo rje tshe/rdo rje kyo ba'i
47b6(2): khyad par gyis//sngags rnam thams cad dgugs pa'i mchog/thams cad rdorje'i dngos zhi ba//rang sngags skyes bu bsgoms nas ni//mi'i bu mo mchog rnams kyi//snying khar rdo rje kyo ba sbyar//
47b7(2): rlung gi dkyil 'khor sbyor ba yin//nges par gartag tu 'gugs par 'gyur//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dga' la ni//rnam par snang mdzad gzugs chen po bsgom/de na sha ci gnas par bsam//
48a1(2): @#//rdo rje mi 'chi sbyor ba yis//lan grangs lnga bcu brjod na ni//nges par gartag tu 'gugs par 'gyur//rdo rje kyo ba gzugs chen po//'bar rdo rje 'dra ba'i 'od//rdo rje 
48a2(2): dkyil 'khor bsgoms na ni//namakha'i bu mo 'gugs pa'i mchog/khro bo rdo rje dam tshig ni//rdo rje sa 'og gnas pa dag/shU la rdo rje kyo ba'i gnas/lham ni bu mo 'gug pa'i gnas//cag gi rdo rgyus

48a6(2): gsungs//gzhan du byas na 'jig par 'gyur//rdo rje sems dpa' rgyal po che//yang dang yang du bskul bar bya//de nyid sngags rnams thams cad kyi//rgyal po mchog tu rtag pa'o//de nas 
48a7(2): bcom ldan 'das namakha' kun rnam par 'phrul ba'i ye shes rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//rdo rje ro thor btsugs gcig ma dam tshig chen po gzhan gyis mi thub pa'i gsung rdo rje 
48b1(2): mchog 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//oM shU li ni swA hA//de nas 'di ni gsungs tsam gyis//klu'i bu mo mthu chen rnams//tshig par 'gyur cing gos dang bral//
48b2(2): sangs rgyas byang chub rjes su dran//sngags kyi rig pa 'di yis ni//klu rnams thams cad 'gug par 'gyur//klu yi bu mo mig bzang dag/bgug na nye bar spyad par bya//de nas bcom  
48b3(2): ldan 'das nam mkha'i dam tshig 'byung ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//chos chen po'i dam tshig rdo rje khro gnyer ma 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsang dang rdo rje thugs rdo rje las phyung go/
48b4(2): oM sarba b+ha ba ya//nA sha ya /trA sa ya trA sa ya/b+he ra Ti b+h+ri ku Ti/ta Ti/bA+ai ra Ti/shwe ta dza Ti ni swA hA//de nasa'adi ni gsungs tsam gyis/ /rig pa 'dzin bu mo kun//ra ba tu 'da' bar gyur nas kyang//
48b5(2): ye shes rdo rje rjes su dran//rig pa 'dzin pa'i ma bu mo mchog/gser gyi rna cha g.yo ba rnams//sngags kyi rig pa 'didang ni//dam tshig la sogs pa yis dgug/'gog pa'i rdo rje rgyal po ni//yongs su
48b6(2): rdzogs pa mdzes mchog gis//ye shes rdo rje gsum las byung//kun nas de ma thag tu 'gugs//yang na khro bo thams cad ni//dben pa'i gnas ni chen po ru//sngags pa'i 'bum du bzlas byas ma//
48b7(2): las rnams thams cad 'byed par bshad//rdo rje slob dpon smod pa dang//theg pa chen po smod pa dag/nan tan tu ni bsad par bya//yang na gnas nas spong bar bya//'di'i byang chub mchog dang ni//
49a1(2): @#//sngags kyi dngos grub thob par 'gyur//zhes bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang chen pos gsungs so//yang gsungs pa//phyogs bcu'i sems can thams cad kyi//lus dang 
49a2(2): ngag sems bsad par ni//dug sems ldan pa'i dgra rnams la//cho ga bzhin du bsgom par bya//khrag dang chu yis gsher ba 'am//bshang gcis gsher bar byas ba'i gos//gos nas lang+ka'i 
49a3(2): rkang pa bsam nan//khro bo'i rgyal po sbyar bar bya//brgya rtsa brgyad ni yongs bzlas na//sangs rgyas kyang ni nges par 'jig/yang gsungs pa//chu yis gos ni gsher bar byas//khro bo mchog 
49a4(2): gis bcings pa yis//lha chen rkang g.yon gyis mnan na//sangs rgyas ngag kyang nges par 'jig/bshang gcis gsher bar byes pa'i gos//ngan pa rul pa dri mnam pa//bgos te sngags ni 
49a5(2): bzlas byas na//skad cig gis bskams 'chi bar 'gyur//thal ba'i skyoms gsher ba'i gos//bsgos pa nas khro bas 'khrugs bzhin du//brgya rtsa brgyad du bzlas n+si ni//rdo rje sem+sa dpa' lnga 'jig par 'gyur//lnga gsung+sa pa//

53a1(2): @#//ba'i phyag rgya bcings nas su//gdug pa'i dgra ni dran par bya//sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' yis//gang zhig sprul par gyur pa yang//mthong ba tsam gyis 'gags 'gyur te//

54a1(2): @#//rdo rje stobs po chen 'gyur ro//ga bur gur kum sbyar ba yis//rilu lcags gsum gyis dkris pa//dbang po gnyis kyis sbyor ba yis//sangs rgyas kun gyis mi

54b1(2): la sogs//gtsug tor 'jog par byed par bshad//khro bo'i nang na de mchog ste//sangs rgyas rdo rje ra ba bsgom pa'o//rig pa'i rgyal po chos mchog rnams//dkon mchog dpal ni nam par

54b7(2): bas//gsung ni gi las kyi bzhin du//las rnams sna tshogs byed par 'gyur//rdo rje chos kyi gzugs chen po//pad+ma ra ga'i 'od 'dra ba//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje la//gnas gsum
