58b6(2): sbyor bas mthong bar 'gyur//kun dga' ra ba skyed mos tshal//lha yi bu mo sogs pas brgyan//sangs rgyas kun gyis byin brlabs pas//bsam gtan dam tshig can gyis mthong//sangs 
58b7(2): rgyas byang chub sems dpa' yis//de ni mngon b.rad bang bskur mthong//rig 'dzin rgyal po chen po yis//mchod pa byas par nges mthong ngo//yang gsungs pa//rdo rje las byung sna tshog+sa pa'i//
59a1(2): @#//rmi lam dri ma med mthong na//sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje las//skyes bu bla med 'grub par 'gyur//gdol pa khyi yi sbyor la sogs//gal te rdo rje blos mthong na//
59a2(2): blo ldan rdo rje sems dpa' yi//sems kyi gnas ni 'grub par 'gyur//de la 'di ni rmi lam rnam par dpyad pa'i dam tshig snying po'o//rang sems rnams ni dmigs pa la//chos rnams thams cad   
59a3(2): rab tu gnas//namakha' rdo rje'i gnas sems 'di//chos med chos nyid med pa'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad ngo mtshar du gyur rmad du gyur nas//de bzhin gshegs 
59a4(2): pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi dam tshig gi the tshom gcod pa rdo rje sems dpa' la zhus pa//bcom ldan 'das 'di ci zhig lags//chos rnams ngo bo med pa la//chos kyi de nyid kun 
59a5(2): bshad pa//e ma'o ngo mtshar las byung ba//namakha' la ni namakha' bsgom//de nas bcom ldan 'da' de bzhin gshegs pa thamscad kyi sku dang gsung dngathugs phyag na rdo rje de bzhin 
59a6(2): gshegs pas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la 'di skad ces gsungs so//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad//nam mkha' ni chos gang dang yang mi ldan 
59a7(2): te//mi ldan pa yang ma yin no//nam mkha' deng ni chos thams cad du gtogs la//thams cad kyi rjes su blta'o snyam du yang mi sems so//de bzhin du bcom ldan 'das de bzhin
59b1(2): gshegs pa thams cad rmi lam lta bu dang//rmi lam gyi dam tshig las byung bar rjes su rtogs par bya'o//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad 'di lta ste//dper na namakha'    
59b2(2): brtag tu med cing bltar mi snang ba thogs pa med pa de bzhin du//bcom ldan 'das de ba zhin gshegs pa thams cad kyang rjes su rtog+sa par bya'o//bcom ldan 'a das de bzhin gshegs pa tha m+sa 
59b3(2): cad|/'di lta ste/dper na lus dang ngag dang sems dang chos thams cad rdo rje'i gnas kyi dam tshig ni//thams cad kyis rjes song//bango bo nyid gcig pa'i phyir la te 'di lta ste sems kyi ngo bo nyid do//
59b4(2): lus dang ngag dang sems kyi khams gang yin pa dang//namakha'i khams de dag gnyis su med cing gnyis su byar med do//bcom ldan 'das de bzhIn gshegs pa thams cad//'di lta ste/dper na sems 
59b5(2): can thams cad namakha'i khams la gnas te//namakha'i khams de yang 'dod pa'i khams na gnas pa yang ma yin/gzugs kyi khams na gnas pa yang mayin//gzugs med pa'i khams na gnas pa  
59b6(2): yang ma yin te//chos gang khams gnas na mi gnas pa de ni skye med do//gang la skyes ba med pa 'di ni chos gang gis kyang brtag par mi nus so//de bas na bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad //
59b7(2): chos thams cad ngo bo nyid med pa'o//kye bcom ldan 'das de bzhinagashegs pa thams cad 'di lta ste//dper na byang chub kyi sems ni de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi ye shes 'byung ba rdo rje'i gnas
60a1(2): @#//byed pa ste/byang chub kyi sems de yang lus la mi gnas sems la mi gnas so//chos gang khams gsum na mi gnas pa de la skyes ba med do//'di ni ye shes 'byung ba rdo rje'i 
60a2(2): gnas yin no//kye bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad//rmi lam de ni bdag gis khams gnas du mi lam mthong bar bya'o snyam du mi sems so//mi yang bdag gis rmi lam mthong ngo snyam 
60a3(2): du mi sems so//khams gsum gyi bya ba de rnams kyang//rmi lam lta bu༐ rmi lam dang 'dra//rmi lam las byung ba'o//de bzhin du bcom ldan 'das de bzhinagashegs pa thams cad//phyogs bcu'i 
60a4(2): 'jig rten gyi khams kyi sangs rgyas dang/byang chub sems dpa' ji snyed pa rnams dang/sems can ji snyed thams cad kyang//mi lam la bdag med pa'i gnas su rjes su rtogs par bya'o//bcom ldan 'das 
60a5(2): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad 'di lta ste/dper na yid bzhin gyi nor bu rin po che ni//rin po che thams cad kyi gtso bo yon tan thams cad dang ldan pa ste/rin po che thams cad kya gtso bo yon tan 
60a6(2): thams cad dang /ldan pa gang yin pa de la/sems can gang dag gis gser ram rin po che 'am dngul yang rung ste//gang bslangs pa de dag thams cad /sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad kyang rjes 
60a7(2): su rtogs par bya'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad rab tu dgyes pa'i spyan gdangs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje de bzhin  
60b1(2): gshegs pa la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das gang nam mkha'i khams kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i chos thams cad la sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams rjes su 'brang ba de ngo mtshar to//de nas sangs rgyas  
60b2(2): dang byang chub sems dpa' de dag thams cad gyis//bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa'i zhabs gnyis la phyag 'tshal nas 'di skad ces gsol to/ /bcom ldan 'das khyed kyis sngag+sa 
60b3(2): thams cad kyi rdo rje'i dngos grub mdor bsdus te gsungs pa//sngags thams cad kyi rdo rje'i mdor bsdus pa'i dngos grub de dag gang la gnas//de nas phyag na rdo rjes de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang 
60b4(2): byang chub sems dpa' de ngag thams cad la legs so zhes bya ba byin nas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la 'di skad ces gsungs so//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad sngags kyi 
60b5(2): dngos grub thams cad ni//sku dang gsung dang thugs la mi gnas so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'on kyang bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad sngags kyi dngos grub thams cad dang/sangs rgyas kyi 
60b6(2): chos thams cad ni bdag gis lus dang ngag dang sems rdo rje la ni gnas so//lus dang ngag dang sems rdo rje de dag kyang//'dod pa'i khams ni mi gnas gzugs kyi khams na mi gnas gzugs med pa'i kham+sa  
60b7(2): na mi gnas//sems kyang lus la mi gnas lus kyang sems la mi gnas//ngag kyang sems la mi gnas sems kyang ngag la mi gnas so//de ci 'i phyir zhe na/namakha' ltar ngo bo nyid dag pa'i phyir ro//
61a1(2): @#//pas gsung rdo rje thams cad kyi dam tshig gi sprin bkod pa zhes bya ba ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//gsung rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//
61a2(2): de nas gsung gi dkyil 'khor mchog/thugs kyi dkyil 'khor rab 'dra ba//dkyil 'khor rnams ni kun gyis mchog/legs par rab tu bshad par bya//khru ni nyi shu'i tshad du bya//gru bzhi lham pa    
61a3(2): cho ga bzhin//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi par//rdo rje bsgoms pas thig gdab po//nyid kyi sku yi dkyil 'khor gnas//rdo rje sku yi yon tan bskyed//rgyal po chen po rdo rje chos//rig pa'i bdag po dbab
61a4(2): par bya//de dbus 'khor lo chen po ni//zlum por kun tu bri bar bya//mdo ru phyag rgya thams cad ni//cho ga bzhin du bri bar bya//tshe dpag med kyi phyag rgya che//de yi dbus su gzhag par bya//rdo rje gnas
61a5(2): mchog de nyid du//thams cad yongs su dkod par bya//yongs su gsal bar cho ga bzhin//dkyil 'khor dam pa byas na ni//de nas gsang ba'i mchod par bya//'on dang rdo rje can dag mnyes//
61a6(2): bshang gci khu ba'i dam tshig gis//mchod na dngos grub thob par 'gyur//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//dam tshig shin tu 'da' dka' ba'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsung gi 
61a7(2): dkyil 'khor ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa//kun nas sprin gyi rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//rab tu gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor 
61b1(2): gsang ba 'di /nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//rdo rje 'dzin mchog gang yin pa'i//gzugs ni dbus su legs bris pa//de'i dkyil 'khor gnas su 'gyur//sku gsung 
61b2(2): thugs kyi gsang las byung//'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje'i gnas ye shes gsang ba yin te rab tu gsang ba'o//de nas phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa 
61b3(2): dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi 'khor lo byung ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi sku dang gsum dang thugs rdo rje'i gsang ba 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung
61b4(2): dang thugs rdo rje las phyung//de la 'di ni rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor gyi sngags kyi snying po'i thig gdab pa'i yi ge'i gnas rnams so//oM AHhUM/rdo rje'i thigs ni gdab pa dang//tshon rtsi dag ni dgyes 
61b5(2): bdag /sngags kyi sems bas mi bya ste//byas na byang chub rnyed par dka'//de bas dam tshig tshul shes pas//sngags kyi lha rnams phab nas su//byin gyis brlabs kyis gnas 
61b6(2): bsgoms la//dkyil 'khor la rnams ni rnam par brtag/rgya chen rnam par snang mdzad dang//yang na spyan ni dbab par bya//sku yi dkyil 'khor gnas yid 'od//rdo rje sku yi yon tan 
61b7(2): 'thob//rdo rje chos ni rgyal po che//rang gi chos mas dbab par bya//'di ni sngags rnams thams cad kyi//gsang bar btu rtag pa yin//rdo rje sems dpa' rgyal po che//mA ma ki 
63a1(2): @#//yang dbab par bya//'di ni sngags rnams thams cad kyi//gsang ba rmad du byung ba'o//de ltar byas na byin rlob cing//yid la dbang ba nyid gshegs te//
63a2(2): ra ba tu dgyes par gyur nas ni//gsang ba dag gis mchog kyang stsol//'di skad kyang gsungs so//sngags kyi sems pas bya ba ni//rdo rje gsang ba rmad po che//khro bo'i rgyal pos 
63a3(2): bkug nas ni//sangs rgyas thams cad mchod par bya//rdo rje dri med gsum dag gi/dam tshig gsum pa dum gsum pa//rdo rje gsum gyi sbyor bas bya//sngags kyi dngos grub 
63a4(2): thob par byed//'di skad kyang gsungs swo//sngags rnams thams cad nyid la yang//rmad du byung ba'i gtor ma dbul//bshad gci sha dang 'bru mar dang//sems las 
63a5(2): byung ba'i tsan dan dang//khu ba dag gis sngags rnams kun//tshim par 'gyur bar kun tu bshad//'di ni dam tshig mchog yin te//sangs rgyas byang chub rdzogs byed pa'o//
63a6(2): thig gdab pa yi cho ga 'di//bdag nyid gang tshe spyad pa na//rnam par snang mdzad bsgoms nas su//rdo rje sems dpa' ra ba tu bsgom//yang na rdo rje mi 'chir grags//slob 
63a7(2): ma rdo rje 'od po che//sangs rgyas kun gyis bstan pa yi//les kyi go 'phang bsgom par bya//sangs rgyas rgyal chen lnga rnams ni//rdo rje'i thig skud dag la dgod//'di ni sangs rgyas   
63b1(2): thams cad kyi/gsang ba rab tu rmad byung ba'o//rnam pa nyi shu rtsa lngar ni//mtshon rtsi dag kyang rnam par bsgom//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//gsang ba byang chub dam pa yin//
63b2(2): sngags rnams thams cad (la) yang//rdo rje hUM ni bsgom par bya//sku dang gsung dang dam tshig mchog/gnas lnga dag tu bsgom par bya//de ltar rtag tu byas na ni//rdo rje mi phyed gsum las
63b3(2): skyes//rdo rje sems dpa' blo ldan pa//'jigs shing skra gnas byin rlob mdzad//rdo rje bum pa gzhag pa ni//sngags rgyud bdag pos shes bya ste//rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin gnas//
63b4(2): brtan pa'i blos ni brtag par bya//dngos grub kun gyi 'bras 'dod pa'i//sngags shes pas ni sbyin sreg bya//bshang gci' bru mar shal sogs//sbyin sreg tu ni dbul bar bya//
63b5(2): rdo rje mi ched gsum dag la//rdo rje gsang ba'i sbyin sreg dbul//dbus su rang gi lha'i gzugs//bsams te zhal du dbab par bya//'di skad kyang gsungs so//dbang po gnyis kyi dam tshig g+si//
63b6(2): sbyin sreg brgya rtsa brgyad du bya//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//dam tshig shin tu 'da' dka' ba'o//rnam snang mdzad kyi sbyor ba yis//slob ma rdo rje gsum las byung//A:ni lus dang 
63b7(2): pa(ra) sems la//bsgom na rdo rje gzung bar bya//rgya chen sems dpa' dang//grags chen rnam par snang mdzad dag/sku gsung thugs kyi dam tshig du//byin gyis brlabs ni rab tu gsol//
64a1(2): @#//de la 'di ni dkyil 'khor chen por 'jug pa'i rdo rje'i dam tshig go/A:kham bir hUM/'di ni dam tshig thams cad kyi lus dang ngag dang sems rdo rje'i snying po'i//de la    
64a2(2): 'di ni rdo rje chen po'i dbang bskur ba'i gsang ba'i ye shes gsang ba'o//sangs rgyas kun gyis nam mkha'i khams//yongs su gang bar rab tu bsgom//rdo rje'i rigs ni Ipa yis//
64a3(2): sil snyan dri yi sprin rnams bsgom//'di ni skad kyang gsungs so//rdo rje sku gsum sngags kyis ni//yungs kar dag gis brdeg par bya//de la de yi chen ni//bdag nyid dbang bskur rab tu stsol//
64a4(2): rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis//yang na sangs rgyas bsgom bar bya//bum pa dam tshig mchog rnams kyis//bzung bar mkhas pa dag gis bsgom//rdo rje rnam par snang mdzad bsam//
64a5(2): slo ba ma rtag tu blo brtan la//lus dang ngag sems rdo rje can//sngags rnams shes ni dgod par bya//de la 'di ni dbang bskur ba thams cad kyi gsang ba slob dpon thams cad kyi rdo rje'i tshig tu smra bar
64a6(2): bya'o//sangs rgyas rnams ni thams cad kyi//rdo rje gsang ba gsum las byung//khams gsum dag gis phyag byas pa//rdo rje dbang bskur chen po sbyin//de la 'di ni slo ba ma thams cad kyis/
64a7(2): rdo rje chen po'i gsol ba bya ba'i chog gsang ba'o//byang chub rdo rjes sangs rgyas la//mchod rten ji ltar bstsal ba bzhin//bdag kyang rab tu bsgral ba'i phyir//nam mkha'i rdo rje deng bdag stsol//
64b1(2): de tshe de la dbang bskur ba//rab tu dges nas stsol bar mdzad//lha yi gzugs kyi sbyor ba yis//snying khar bdag po gzhag par bya//rdo rje slob ma blo ldan la//sngags kyi yi ge'i tshig byin la//
64b2(2): dkyil 'khor dag ni bstan par bya//sangs rgyas kun gyis gsungs pa yi//dam tshig gsang ba bsgrag par bya//khyod kyis srog chags bsad par bya//brdzun gyi tshig kyang smra bar bya//ma byin 
64b3(2): par yang khyod kyis long//bud med dag kyang bsten par bya//rdo rje'i lam ni 'di dag ni//sems can thams cad bskul bar bya//'di skad kyang gsungs te//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//
64b4(2): dam tshig mchog tu rtag pa'o//sngags kyis bskul ba smras pa yis//de la sngags kyang sbyin par bya//sngags kyi rgyal po'i ting 'dzin dang//gsang sbyin pa brtsams par bya//khu ba 'am yang na 
64b5(2): bshad ba'ang rung //cho ga bzhin du sngags nas ni//rdo rje'i blos ni bza' bar bya'o//'on tang dngos grub rnyed mi dka'//rnam pa 'di bzhi gsang chen te//rdo rje can ni kun gyi gsang//da sngags kyi 
64b6(2): 'khor lo bud med gzugs//sems can don spyod phyir gnas pa'o//de la 'di ni rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor thams cad kyi sngags kyi mnyes par bya ba gsang ba'o//glang po'i sha dang rta'i sha/sha chen dag kyang 
64b7(2): bza' bya zhing//sngags rnams kun la dbul bar bya//de ltar byas na mgon po mnyes//nyin re rdo rje slob ma la//mkhas pas dkyil 'khor bstan par bya//bshad gci sha yi bya ba dang//rdo rje gsang 
65a1(2): ka @#//ba'i tshig dag gis//sngags rnams kun gyi yi ge oM//bsgoms na de ma theg tu 'bar//bcom ldan 'das sngags kyi rig pa'i skyes bu chen pos de skad ces bka' stsal to//
65a2(2): dngos grub thams cad sgrub pa dang//dam tshig chen po grub pa dang//sangs rgyas byang chub nyid dag kyang//nan tan du ni bsgrub par bya//mi snang stobs dang brtson 'grus dang//rdo rje dgug pa'i mchog kyang//
65a3(2): rdo rje sku yi bka' bzhin du//dkyil 'khor gyi dag gis thams cad 'gyur//bshad gci dang ni sha chen rnams//cha ni mnyam par byas nas su//kham phar sbyar mar bzhag na ni//sangs rgyas kun dang lhan 
65a4(2): cig cig 'khod//'di skad ces kyang gsungs te//de la 'di ni gsang ba thams cad kyi rdo rje mngag gzhug pa rab tu sgrub pa'i tshig gi go/dri ni 'bar ba'i 'od bzang po//nam mkha' rdo rje'i dbus su   
65a5(2): bsam//sangs rgyas kun gyis nam mkha' rdo rje khams//yongs su gang bar rnam par bsgom//de yi sku gsung thugs kyi gnas//der ni sngags kyis da bab par bya//de la 'di ni sku 
65a6(2): dang gsung dang thugs kyi sngags rdo rje byin gyis rlob pa'i snying po'o//AHkha d+hi//phyag na rdo rje'i gzugs chen dang//phyag na pad+mo 'od chen dang//gzhan gyis mi thub gzugs chen dag/bsgoms nas gsang ba'i gan+sa 
65a7(2): dag go//de la 'di ni rdo rje gsang ba'i gnas so//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//mi bskyod pa ni rab tu brtag/tshe dpag med pa'i 'khor lo che//rdo rje snang mdzad de bzhin te/'od chen sdug bsngal 
65b1(2): chen po yis//thams cad snying pos bskul bar bya//de la 'di ni rdo rje'i snying po thams cad bskul ba'o//AHbam dun rtse che dang rdo rje che//lcags kyu sna tshogs mchog rnams kyis//rdo rje cho ga bzhin  
65b2(2): bskul na//sangs rgyas byang chub rab tu 'grub//'di skad ces kyang gsungs so//nyams dga' ba yi ri dag dang//chu gling rnam pa sna tshogs sku//zla ba phyed ni tshun chad kyis//sangs rgyas zhing 
65b3(2): du 'gro thob pa gdon mi za//rir bsum cu rtsa drug gi/rdul phran ji snyed yod pa yi//de yi 'khor tshogs lhan cig tu//'khor lo can gyi byang chub 'grub//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//
65b4(2): sangs rgyas zhing du 'gro bar 'gyur//zab mo'i chos kyang thob 'gyur zhing //sangs rgyas sa yang 'thob par 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das dam tshig rdo rje bzhad pas de skad ces bka' stsal to//de la 'di ni 
65b5(2): mngag gzhug pa thams cad kyi gsang ba'i rdo rje gsang ba'o//rdo rje sems dpa' ye shes che//de bzhin gsung ba gi rdo rje 'dzin//rdo rje sku yi dgongs pa che//mngag gzhug pa dag rtag tu bskul//
65b6(2): de la 'di ni rdo rje ye shes 'khor lo sngags kyi sa dam tshig gi gnas so//dam tshig bskul ba dang//dam tshig gtong ba dang//dam tshig bod pa dang//dam tshig bcing ba'o//kha dbyings rgya chen dag ni//
65b7(2): chos rnams thams cad rnam par spangs//gong bu'i tshul gyis byed pa rdo rje rmad byung gzugs can no//bcom ldan 'das sangs rgyas thams cad kyi bu gcig pa rdo rje sems dpa' chen pos de skad ces bka' 
66a1(2): @#//stsal to//sangs rgyas rdo rje sems dpa' rung //gal te dbang du bya 'dod na//rdo rje mchog gsum 'dzin pa che//gsang ba chen po 'di bsam mo//rdo rje 'jam pa stobs po che//
66a2(2): namakha'i rdo rje'i dbus su bsam//mnga' lnga'i sbyor bdag gis ni/dbu rgyan rtse mo yang dag pa spro//gnas lnga dag tu sngags shes pas//rdo rje khro bos rab tu dbab//sngags shing rgyal bar bsgom pa ni//byis 
66a3(2): pa blo ni grags chen gyis//bsam gtan 'di ni zla phyed du//gsal ba'i bskul bdag gis bsgom//sngags rnams kun gyis rab gsang ba//rdo rje don gyis blo yis gsungs//sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor mchog
66a4(2): dag ni//namakha' rdo rje'i dbus bsam pa//sngags kyi rdo rje hUM la sogs//rdo rje gsum la sogs par bsgom//so ni mig gi dbus bsams na//'di dag cho ga bzhin du brtan//sngags rnams kun gyi gzug+sa 
66a5(2): dag ni//sku gsum rdo rje can du mthong //bkres skom la sogs nyon mongs che//sbyor ba 'di ni rnams bsams na//rdo rje thugs kyi bka' bzhin du//sdug bsngal thams cad byang bar 'gyur//rnam
66a6(2): par snang mdzad gzugs chen po//bsams na don thams cad 'byor//baM ni kha yi nang du bsgom//oM ni lce la 'dug par bsam//bza' bar bya ba kun gyi gnas//yid bzhin nor bus brgyan 
66a7(2): pa ste//zhi ba sdug bsngal thams cad sel//ye shes rdo rje las byung ba'o//bcom ldan 'das yid bzhin gyi nor bus 'di dag skad ces bka' stsal pa//de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de bzhin
66b1(2): gshegs pa dpa' bo chen po rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pas//rdo rje bsgom pa'i tshig gis mchog rdo rje'i gsung las byung ngo//bI sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor 'od bzang po//namakha' maM rdo rje'i dbus su bsam//rdo rje sku 
66b2(2): gsum sbyor ba yis//bskyed de 'di ni rnam par bsam//rgyan rnams thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa//cung zad sel ba rdo rje 'gyid//zhi ba thor tshugs dbu rgyan can//bsams na thams cad brtsam par 
66b3(2): bya//rdo rje dpa' bo rlangs kyi 'phreng ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kun nas dbyangs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzinal snyoms par zhugs nas//rdo rje chen po bsgom pa'i dam tshig 
66b4(2): 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las byung ngo//tsuM/nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dam pa ni//nam mkha' rdo rje'i dbus su bsam/sangs rgyas sprin gyis cho ga yis//rdo rje gsum gyi grags chen ni//
66b5(2): lus dang ngag dang sems la dbab//bskul byed rdo rje ma bsgom mo//rgyan rnams tsad thams cad yongs su rdzogasapa//kha dog dkar po rab tu bsgom//rdo rje sems dpa' rgyal po che//bsams la sngag+sa 
66b6(2): kyis tshig dgod do//rdo rje'i dam tshig ye shes kyi 'od gzer zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzinato//de nas bcom ldan 'dis phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa//bsam pa thams cad rdo rje longs spyod Ipa zhes bya ba'i ting 
66b7(2): nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/ting nge 'dzin gyi 'khor lo'i tshul 'di//nyid kyi sku dang daM gsung dang thugs rdo rje la phyung ngo//ji/sangs rgyas dkyil 'khor dam pa dag/nam mkha'i dbyings kyi   
67a1(2): @#//dbus su bsam//sangs rgyas kun gyi cho ga yis//rdo rje bsgoms pas dbab par bya//dzam+b+ha la ni nor sgrub pa//gnod spyin chen po sbyin bskyed par bya//zhi ba gnod sbyin gzugs 
67a2(2): 'dzin pa//thor tshugs dbu rgyan rdo rje can//sangs rgyas ngal yi cho ga yis//gnas lnga dag tu bsgom par bya//rdo rje bdud rtsi'i chu de yis//bsam gtan gnas la 'dug la dbul//rdo rje sems 
67a3(2): dpa'i cho ga yis//dbu rgyan de la rnam par bsam//de ltar byas na gnod sbyin dbang //gnod pa gnas dbang po dam 'od chen mnyes//rdo rje dam tshig gi phyag rgya rdzas sgrub pa'i tog gi dpal zhes bya ba'i 
67a4(2): ting nge 'dzin to//de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa//rdo rje 'dod pa nye bar longs spyod pa'i dpal zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//gnod sbyin mo'i rdo rje'i dam 
67a5(2): tshig 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las 'byung ngo//k+ShiM /mkha' dbyings rdo rje'i dbus gnas par//gru bzhi lham par ba tu bzang //thams cad rin chen bzhi las byung//me tog spos kyis 
67a6(2): kun tu 'khrigs//gnod sbyin mo ni thams cad kyis//namakha'i dbyings ni gang bar bsam//gzugs kyi sbyin dag rnams bsams la//rdo rje gsum gyi spyod pas yi//gzugs ni gcig tu 
67a7(2): bsam par bya//lus gsum rdo rje'i bye brag gis//de yi bsam gtan sgom par bya//rdo rje 'jam pa'i ting nge 'dzin gnas//dbu rgyan la ni khro bo bsgom//snying khar sngags kyi tshig 
67b1(2): bsgom la//rdo rje'i sbyor ba brtsam par bya//gnod spyin mo thams cad dang mnyam pa nyid du gnas pa bsgom pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//de nas bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa//
67b2(2): rdo rje'i sngags thams cad dngos grub kyi rnam par 'phrul pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge'adzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//dngos grub phra mo 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//sku dang  
67b3(2): gsung thugs yang dag 'grub//sangs rgyas gzugs 'dzin 'od bzang po//dzam+bu chu bo'i gser 'od 'dra//dngos grub phra mo dag la gnas//mi snang la sogs yang dag grub//'jam pa'i 'od 
67b4(2): dang 'dra bar 'gyur//gnod spyin rgyal po grub pa che//rig 'dzin dag gi dbang por 'gyur//de la 'di ni rdo rje dngos grub thams cad kyi gzugs gsang ba'i dngos grub rnams so//sngags kyi dngos 
67b5(2): grub thob pa yis//mdzes pa'i gzugs rnams thams cad ni//'jig rten khams ni thams cad du//mthong ba nyid kyis dga' bar 'gyur//gtsug tor thams cad gyur pa ni//yid 
67b6(2): bzhin nor bu'i bdag por 'gyur//sangs rgyas byang chub byed pa'i mchog /sangs rgyas rdo rje rab sgom pa'o//bcom ldan 'das bsam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'i 
67b7(2): rdo rjes de skad ces bka' stsal to//de nas phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag pos//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang    
70a1(2): @#//kyi lam gang rnams//byed pa 'jigs shing mi sdug pa//sangs rgyas byang chub bston pa ste//sku yi rdo rje nyid du 'gyur//de nas phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag po(sa)//
70a2(2): drag po'i dam tshig 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//khams gsum dag na gnas pa yi//bud med rdo rje las byung rnams//sna tshogs dngos pos sbyar ba ni//dam tshig mchog
70a3(2): tu rmad byung ba'o//de nas phyag na de('i) bzhin gshegs pa thams can kyi bdag pos//khyab 'jug gi dam tshig 'di//nyid kyi ska dang gsung dang thugs rdorje las phyung ngo//sems can las 
70a4(2): byung ji snyed pa//dbyer med sku dang gsum la gnas pa//rdo rje mkha' dbyings bdag nyid kyang //bsam gtan rdo rjes bsad par bya//de nas phyag na rdo rjede bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag po(sa)/
70a5(2): rang gi dam tshig gi rdo rje 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsang dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//sku yi rdo rje tshangs par 'gyur//gsung gi rdo rje dbang phyug che//thugs kyi rgyal po rdo rje 'dzin//de nyid khyab

70a7(2): khrag dang sha ni za ba dang//rtag tu gzhan gyi bud med spyod//ngam tshig dga' ba 'di rnams kyis//rdo rje chen po mnyes par bya//de nas phyag na daE(da)de bzhin gshegs pa thams ( )cad kyis bdag po(sa)//
70b1(2): klu'i rgyal po thams cad kyi dam tshig 'di//nyid sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//'o ma 'thung ba phra ma can//de dag 'dod dang dri la' bab//dam tshig 'di ni bsgrub par bya//gzhan du nyes pa nyon   
70b2(2): mongs 'gyur//de nas phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bdag pos//lham yin kyi bu mo'i dam tshig 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rde rje las phyung ngo//khro zhing nga rgyal gsod pas 
70b3(2): non//spos dang me tog spyod la dgyes//rdo rje sa 'og dam tshig ste//gdul dka' rdo rje 'jigs byed pa'o//de nas phyag na de bzhin gshegs pa dathams cad kyi bdag pos//srin mo rnams kyi dam tshig

70b5(2): de nas phyag na rdo rje de bzhIn gshegs pa thams cad kye bdag pos//rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma thams cad kyi dam tshig 'di nyid sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi rdo rje las phyung ngo//bshang gci dang ni khrag dang dag dang//
70b6(2): chang la sogs pa'ang rtag tu btung//rdo rje mkha' 'gro sbyor ba yis///gnas kyi mtshan nyid kyis bsad bya//ngo bo nyid kyis kun 'byung ba//khams gsum dag na rnam par spyod//sems can  
70b7(2): kun la phan 'dod ba'i//dam tshig ma lus spyad par bya//khams gsum pa thams cad rdo rje'i dam tshig gi rjes su 'brang ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin to//de nas phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 
72a1(2): @#//dam tshig ste//khros pa rnams kyis mi thub po//de nas phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag pos//sngags 'chang las dang po pa thams cad kyi dam tshig 
72a2(2): 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//sku yi rdo rje kha zas la phyi rol rdo rje 'dzin pa dang //mdo gdon cho ga'i bya ba la//rtag par rdo rje chos su bya//bcom ldan 'das ngo bo nyid dag 
72a3(2): pa rdo rjes de skad ces bka' stsal to//de nas phyag na rdo rje da bzhin gshegs pa thams can kyi bdag pos//rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thus kyi rdo rje mnyam pa nyid rnam par dgongs 
72a4(2): nas cang mi gsung bar gyur to//de nas yang brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing ri rab kyi rdul du rab phra ba dang /mnyam pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnam+sa 
72a5(2): kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad la phyag 'tshal nas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi bdag po 'di ci'i slad du//de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
72a6(2): cad dang byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi nang du cang mi gsung bar bzhugs//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin bshegs pa thams cad kyis//byang chub sems dpa' de dag thams cad la 'di skad 
72a7(2): ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang bsung dang thugs kyi bdag po 'di sku dang sa gsung dang thugs rdo rje'i ngo bo nyid na dmigs su med pa'i yi ge'i tshig la//
72b1(2): ngo bo nyid med pa'i yi ge'i tshig brjod nas//cang mi gsung bar bzhugs so//rigs kyi bu de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bdag po 'di sems pa ni 'di snyam du 'gyur to//sku ni mi 'gyur ma skyes
72b2(2): pa//gsung dang thugs kyang mtshan ma med//nam mkha'i rdog las byung//log par bzung bas zin pa med//de nas 'jam dpal la sogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyis//
72b3(2): de bzhin gshegs pa de dag la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad//gsung rdo rje'i tshig la phyin la ci log kun 'byung bar mi rtag du gsol//de ci'i slad du zhe na//

72b5(2): tshangs pa la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' chen po//mngon par shes pa chen po'i ye shes thob pa rnams chos thams cad kyi mtshan nyid kyi ngo bo nyid kyi mi shes nas 'di snyam du rnam par rtog ste//ci rdo rje'i
72b6(2): bdag nyid 'di de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje'i de kho na nyid yongs su shes nas//gsang ba'i yi ge rnams 'chang ngam snyam du sems pa yang mchis so//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin
72b7(2): gshegs pa thams cad kyis//byang chub sems dpa' de dag la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//byang chub sems dpa' khyod rnams lta ci smos te//de bzhin gshegs pa thabs cad kyis//

73a2(2): nyid med pa mi 'gyur ba las byung ngo//rigs kyi bu sems can chen po'i khongs su gtogs pa'i sems can ji snyed yod pa de dag thams cad byang chub la gnas pa sangs rgyas rdo rje'o//de ci'i (mi)slad du zhe na//
73a3(2): kye ma seMs can de dag ni sku dang gsum rdo rje'i chos nyid kyi phyir//sku dang gsung ('a)thugs rdo rje'i ye shes thob pa'o//de nas phyag na rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdorje'i bdag pos//de bzhin

73a5(2): byed pa smra ma yin//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thamscad kyis//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa phyag na rdo rje chen po la phyag 'tshal nas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom

73b1(2): rdo rje la gnas so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis slar gsol pa//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje'i dngos grub dang //khams gsum thams 
73b2(2): cad gang na gnas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag po ye shes rdo rje'i bdag pos gsungs pa//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi dngos grub dang //

73b4(2): gang na yang ma yin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' de dag ngo mtshar du gyur rmad du gyur nas//e ma'o rdo rje e rdo rje//e ma'o rdo rje yi ge bshad pa//gang du lus dang ngag sems med//de ru gzugs
73b5(2): ni rnam par bsgom//de nas ston pa rdo rje 'dzin//sangs rgyas kun gyis phyag byas nas//rdo rje gsum mchog mchog chen mchog/rdo rje gsum gyi dbang phyug mchog/rig pa'i skyes bu bsgom pa

74a1(2): @#//'di ni rnam par bsam//gzhan du rnam par bsgoms na ni//de dag dngos grub mchog mi 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das rig pa'i skyes bu rdo rje gsang bas de skad ces bka' stsal to//
74a2(2): khams gsum las byung rgyal mo che//mnyes par byed ma rnams bsams nas//rdo rje sku gsum gyis mtshan ma//khyu mchog de ni mnyes par 'gyur//gang zhig 'di dag sgom pa yi//byang chub 
74a3(2): sems dpa' grags chen rnams//zhag bdun gyis ni gzi che ba'i//sku gsum dngos grub thob par 'gyur//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje bsdus pa'i gsang 
74a4(2): ba 'di//nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rda rje las phyung ngo//sku gsung thugs kyi dam tshig ni//phyag na rgya chen po'i don brtags pa//cho ga bzhin du kun bsgoms na//skad cig gis ni

75a1(2): @#//pa thams cad kyi//sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje de bzhin gshegs pa//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi nyi mnyam pa nyid la gnas pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par 
75a2(2): zhugs so//snyoms par zhugs nas kyang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor la gzigs gnas cang mi gsung bar gyur to//de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 
75a3(2): byams pas//de bzhin gshegs pa thabs cad la phyag 'tshal nas 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thamscad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje'i gsang ba gsang ba 'dus 
75a4(2): par|/mngon par dbang bskur ba'i slob dpon la//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang//byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis ji ltar blta bar bgyi//rigs kyi bu de bzhin gshegs pa tham+sa

75a6(2): kyi bu mdor bstanan//phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams na sangs rgyas (ra) byang chub sems dpa' ji snyed 'khod cing 'chol skyong ba de dag thams cad dus gsum du byon nas//slob dpon de la de bzhin gshegs 
75a7(2): pa thams cad kyi mchod pas yang dag par mchod nas//sangs rgyas kyi zhing der yang 'gro zhing dag rdo rje'i yi ge yang 'di skad du 'byin to//bdag cag de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi pha'o//bdag cag  
75b1(2): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ston pa'o/zhes bya ba'i bar du'o//'di lta ste dper na rigs kyi bu sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das phyogs bcu na ji snyed bzhugs pa dang//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasade rnams 
75b2(2): kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las byung ba'i bsod nams kyi phung po//ji snyed yod pa de bas kyang slob dpon gyi ba spu'i bu ga gcig gi bsod nams khyad par du 'phags so//de ci'i phyir zhe na//rigs kyi bu 
75b3(2): byang chub kyi sems ni sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyi snying por gyur pa'o//skye bo'i gnas su 'gyur pa nas thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi 'byung gnas yin pa'i bar du'o//de nas yang byang chub sems dpa' byams 
75b4(2): pa yid 'jigs shing skrag nas cang mi smra bar gyur to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa mi bskyod pa dang//de bzhin gshegs pa'i dkon mchog dpal dang//de bzhin gshegs ba 'od dpag du med pa dang//de bzhin

175b3(2): sku sogs rdo rje gsum 'di ni//'jig rten khams rnams kun tu sbyor//yid rten bde gcig bsnyen pa ste//de nyid gsum mnyam med pa ste//bsten te dam tshig gcig gyur pa//ye  
175b4(2): shes nye bar bsgrubs pas pa'o//yid rton mnyam pa'i sbyor ba las//sgrub ba yis ni࿒dam tshig 'gyur//'bras bu mngon sum gyis sbyor ba//bsgrub pa chen po bzhi po'o//rkyen 
175b5(2): dang nyan thos mdo ste pa//'di dag ldan pa rab tu sngags//sems dang dmigs dang 'di sbyor//skad cig gis kyang nges 'grub 'gyur//bsnyen pa'i dam tshig sbyor gcig dang//nye bar 
175b6(2): sgrub par gang gyur pa//sgrub pa dam tshig nyid dang ni//sgrub pa chen po bzhi pa yis//'di las yang dag 'jug 'gyur/bar//ji ltar ji ltar 'jig rten tshogs//lha nyid tu ni  
175b7(2): rab tu brtag/chos kyi dbye bas shes par bya//rdo rje ye shes bsgrub pa'o//bsnyen pa'i dam tshig yang dag sbyor//byang chub mchog gi ni rnam par bsgom//gzugs dang sgra dang 
176a1(2): @#//de bzhin dri//ro dang reg bya chos rnams la//dngos grub mchog gi rnam spyad pa//de ni nye bar bsgrub par 'gyur//sngags bdag gang yin bsgom gnas ni//bskul ba  
176a2(2): bsgrub par 'gyur ba yin//rang sngags rdo rje can gyi gzugs//de ni bsgrub pa chen por 'dod//bsnyen pa rdo rje bdud rtsi bdag/sngags pas kun tu brtag par bya//dngos grub 'dod pa'i bsngag+sa
176a3(2): pas ni//dbu rgyan la ni mchog gang bsam//'di ni sngags rnams thams cad kyi//de nyid gsum gsang yang dag 'byung//sngags kyi de nyid don gnyer ba'i//bsgrub pa pos ni dben pa bsten/
176a4(2): sna tshogs lung gi yang dag rtog//sngags ni dgon pa chen por 'dod//de yi dpag bsam shing longs spyod//sa phyogs zhes ni yongs su bsgrags//mkha' dang mnyam pa'i sbyor ba ni//rnam par 
176a5(2): dben pa chen por bshad//ri bo rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas dang//de rigs dngos med las byung ba'o//de 'dra'i dben pas mtshon pa ru//der ni 'dod pa chen rnam gnas te//gnas med gnas pa'i sbyor 
176a6(2): ba yis//rtag tu dngos grub thob par 'gyur//bcom ldan rdo rje bsgrub pa ni//chen pos de skad bka' stsal to//dgon pa'i sa phyogs gtsang ma sogs//'jig rten pa yang smra ba gsal//
176a7(2): bsnyen pa sku yi brtul zhugs mchog/blo dang ldan pa'i rdo rje bzhis//rdo rje gsum med sbyor bas//bsgom na dngos grub thob par 'gyur//me long zhes byang gon po che//rin chen zhes bya 
176b1(2): dben pa'o//chen po zhes bya 'ong dpag med//don yod pa ni ri bor yin//chung sgo dag pa'i chos kyi che//gnas lnga dag tu rab tu brtag/ji srid don ni sbyor ba bzhis//yi ge oM ni sdom 
176b2(2): par 'gyur//ji ltar nam mkha' mtshan nyid gcig/rang gi mtshan nyid dor mi byed//de bzhin de nyid gsum bzhi byed//yi ge oM gcig bsdom pa'o//gnas med gnas pa zhes bya'o//
176b3(2): oM gcig gis ni 'gro pa khyab//de nyid mi bsal sbyor ba'o//gtso bo rang nyid bzlogs pa yin//la la glang po spangs nas ni//rjes nas dbyibs tshol bar 'dod//de bzhin ma 'ongs bsngags 
176b4(2): pa ni//oM las sdom (ba)gzhan pa (ba)du 'dod//gcig gnyis su gsum dang bzhi dang lnga//dus thub de bzhin nor can dang//phan tshun sbyor gnas brjod pa las//bsam mi khyab pa'i grang+sa 
176b5(2): su 'gyur//bcom ldan gcig kyang dum ste//tshig tu brjod pa rnam sar spangs//brda tsam du ni oM zhes bya//dngos med sbyor ba'i chos nyid yin//chos dang long spyod sprul pa ste//sku gsum du 
176b6(2): yang yang dag bshad//gsum po yang dga' 'dzin pas na//des ni oM ni sdom pa yin//dngos po cig bu mi bskyod dgong//de nyid sdom par gyur pa yin//brda ru brjod pa sna tshogs pa//bsaM 
176b7(2): nas sdom pa kun tu bshad//mi sha phyed nam mkha'i sbyor pa yin//bsam dang 'don dang rnam par bral//rten 'brel rig las yang dag byung//rje bcas zhes bya de bsgom bya//rtag tu 'byung 
177a1(2): @#//ba'i ye shes bzhag/dmigs la sog pa bdun rgyal ba//yas dang cig shos zla phyed ste//shes rab tha bas kyang sgrub par bya//'di dag zung gyur zla ba ste//
177a2(2): rdo rje mid can dang phyed dang gnyis//de ni sbyor ba de nyid kyis//dngos grub myur du tho ba par 'gyur//zhag ni thu ba ba zla phyed dang//zla phyed zla ba phyed gnyis min//'jig rten pa yi zhag dbye bas//rgya chen  
177a3(2): ngas ni bshad pa yin/gsang ba'i mchog las byung ba ni//zla ba phyed kyi sgrub par gsungs//de nyid nye bar sgrub bshad pa'i//sgom pa'i yul ni dam pa yin//gang tshe shes rab thabs 
177a4(2): ye shes//de bzhin nyid shes las byung ba//gsang rgyas sku 'dzin dpal dang ldan//rdo rje mi phyed 'od po che//byang chub skad cig gis sbyor ba//rdo rje skus ni byed par 'gyur//srog les gas dang 
177a5(2): grangs mnyam pa'i//dpag med phyogs bzhugs sangs rgyas rnams//rdo rje mi phyed gsum bsgoms pa//rdo rje byin rlabs mdzad par 'gyur//'das dang ma 'ongs sangs rgyas dang//gang zhig phyogs 
177a6(2): bcur bzhugs pa rnams//chos sku mchog gis rnal 'byor pa//sangs rgyas de rnams byin gyis rlob//'das pa rnams kyi chos sku yis//khams mi khyab ba'i phyogs na zhugs//sngags mchog 
177a7(2): bsam gyis mi khyab pas//bkod pa rnams kyi byin gyis rlob//sangs rgyas gsum la dbye ba ni//cung zad tsam yang yod ma yin//'od kyang dngos med las byung ba'i//sngags pa rnams 
177b1(2): la byin gyis rlob//phyogs bcur bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//mi phyed rdo rje gsum bsgoms byas//sku ni de lta bur mtshan pa//de ltar byin gyis slob par mdzad//rig dang mi rig yang 
177b2(2): dag ldan//srogs la sogs te bcu gnyis bshad//dus kyi nyis gyur lam la sogs//de nyid zla ba la sogs 'gyur//zla ba la sogs grangs mnyam pas//nyi ma bcu yi ming can yin//
177b3(2): khya ba 'jug de ni dbye ba yis//lam brtson pa ni rtag pa yin//de nyid de ni bsgrub pa mchog/sdom bu yul zhes bshad pa yin//sngags dang dngos med de byed pa//gte 'khor lo bskor dang mtshungs//

177b5(2): rkang par yang dag ldan//'dzin pa dag gis mi nus pa//lta dang nyan dang dri dang ro//reg bya rnams ni dngos med pa//rang bzhin sems med gyur ba yis//'jim gzugs sangs rgyas sku can 
177b6(2): te//rang gi chos ni spangs nas su//'on kyang 'gro ba'i bla ma'i skur//sangs rgyas byin gyis brlabs kyis//de bzhin sngags pa bdag nyid can//dngos med rdo rje'i sku nyid yin//shing 
177b7(2): dang sogs dang rin chen sogs//rang gi lha yi gzugs byas te//rang gi chos ni spang nas su//tshig gi lam dang stong byed pa//sku gsung thugs dang ldan pa mchog/cho 'phrul de la yang 
178a1(2): @#//dag byung//de nas dang sogs yang dag pas//slar yang de la gsol 'debs pas//gsol ba 'debs pa tsam gyis ni//de dang 'dra ga la 'gyur//phyi rol 
178a2(2): chos la gang zhig dga'//lta rnams thams cad spong bar byed//de ltar sku la sku la byung ba//dpal ldan chos 'di 'ang gsang gi lam//byin brlabs pa dag longs spyod pan//gsum gi rdo rje  
178a3(2): can du byed//'das dang ma byon sangs rgyas rnams//phyogs bcu na ni rnam bzhugs pa//byung dang byung bar 'gyur rnams ni//mi phyed gsungs gi lam du 'gyur//yid rton bde ba bzhi bdag
